- **Project Queue redesign — filter chips + flat table + inline row expansion (the glance surface).** The Queue screen dropped its 3-column master/detail/actions grid (`grid-cols-[30%_35%_35%]` — the list got 30% of the width and a row showed zero quantitative data until clicked) for a glance-first layout: four toggleable status-bucket **filter chips** with live counts (`running` / `attention` / `queued` / `complete`; hidden buckets persist in `localStorage` under `autodev_queue_hidden_buckets`; all visible by default; reorder mode bypasses filters + grouping so drags always see the raw queue), a **full-width table** (PROJECT | STATUS | PHASE | PROGRESS | ELAPSED | COST | QUEUED, rows grouped running → attention → queued-by-position → complete-newest-first), and a **per-row inline expansion** that reuses `QueueProjectSnapshot` + `QueueActionHub` verbatim (`selectedId` → `expandedId`; every per-state action — escalation command panel, stopped recovery, depends-on, revalidate, remove, relaunch — is unchanged). **Server:** `GET /api/queue` now attaches a uniform summary block to EVERY entry (was ACTIVE-only W3-B): `phases_total`/`phases_complete` (roadmap checkbox stats), `cost_total` + `duration_seconds` (per-phase **active work** summed from the project's `metrics.jsonl` — deliberately not run_summary calendar wall-clock), `current_phase_raw_id` (live entry from `pipeline_state`; parked entry from `parked_state_snapshot`; COMPLETED/FAILED from the last deduped metrics phase), and `parked_agent` (`None`-tolerant for pre-existing snapshots). The aggregation lives in a new shared pure helper `_project_metrics_totals` (read + dedup-keep-last + sums), extracted from `/api/metrics-summary` — whose response is **byte-identical** (golden guard + all 34 pre-existing metrics tests green, the four row-math helpers hoisted to module scope). `GET /api/queue/{id}/snapshot` gains `cost_total`/`duration_seconds`, **reviving a dead UI line** (`snap.cost_total > 0` at the snapshot's background details — the field was never sent before). **Orchestrator:** `_queue_park_active_entry` snapshots `current_agent` (display-only — the parked row's PHASE cell reads "UI-E2 · reviewer"; the revival restore cherry-picks explicit keys and keeps hard-coding `"escalation"`, verified). **Also fixes a live latent bug:** queue-row clicks called the undefined `setHubModalCommand(null)` → a ReferenceError on every row click; removed with the row rewrite. ELAPSED semantics per state: running = wall-clock since `started_at`; parked = time **waiting** since `parked_at`; complete = summed active work; failed = `relTime(failed_at)`; cost `0`/`null` renders as an em-dash (zero-suppression house rule). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 22 + 23 + 1 new, 4 rewritten, 8 updated, 2 files deleted):* new `tests/test_queue_summary_enrichment.py` (helper semantics incl. the dedup-keep-last + zeroed-dict edges, uniform block per state, parked/stale/ingested rows, snapshot fields, metrics-summary golden guard) and `tests/test_ui_queue_table_redesign.py` (grid removed, chips + persistence + reorder bypass + bucket mapping/grouping, column headers, expansion toggle/effect, per-state cell formats, zero-suppression, DEP/waiting/skip/drag/ranks carried over, `fmtUSD` hoist); `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_queue.py` `_SNAPSHOT_KEYS` drift-gate + park/snapshot fixtures extended with `current_agent`; `tests/test_w3b_queue_phases_progress.py` ACTIVE-only assertions inverted (`…_does_not_get_phases_fields` → `…_gets_phases_fields`, `test_only_active_entry_enriched_in_mixed_queue` → `test_all_entries_enriched_in_mixed_queue`, no-roadmap case now keys-present-as-None); **deleted** `tests/test_w4a_queue_time_in_state.py` + `tests/test_w4b_queue_phase_progress.py` (asserted the removed row secondary-text markup and the ACTIVE-only gate — superseded by the ELAPSED/PROGRESS cell tests); anchor updates in `test_w4c`/`test_w4d`/`test_w4i` (`// ── Column 3:` → `// ── Row expansion: Action Hub`), `test_ui_phase5_waiting_for_parent.py` (rows region markers), `test_ui_escalation_command_regressions.py` (`[expandedId, snapshotVersion]`). Suites: queue 152 / metrics 34 / orchestrator-queue 69 / scrub+run-totals 11 / UI sweep 849 green (the only failures are the 2 pre-existing `test_ui_design_system_restrictions` shadow-xl ones, which fail on clean HEAD); JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/index.html`, `ui/server.py`, `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` (+ the test files above). *Callouts (not actioned):* `nextEligible` in `QueueScreen` is write-only dead state (outside this change's scope); expanding a synthetic `ingest-*` row still 404s its snapshot fetch (pre-existing); no caching on the new per-entry file reads (+2 small reads/entry per 5s poll — fine at current queue sizes, revisit past ~50 entries); `/api/completion-report` stays active-project-only (completed-row report access remains on the Monitor); `parked_agent` for ESCALATION rows is typically `"escalation"` (the agent at park time) — copy nuance for a later visual pass.
  **Round 3 (per-state expansion designs).** The expansion stopped being one shape reused for every row and became a purpose-fit body per state. **Active:** dropped the redundant "Started" (≈ Elapsed) and "Preflight passed" lines — a running project is self-evidently valid. **Ready/queued:** reclaimed the wasted space with a confidence note built from the formerly-dead `nextEligible` ("Next up — starts automatically when the pipeline is free" / "Press Run next project to start it" / "N projects ahead of it in line") and a state-appropriate stat trio — **Phases planned / Preflight (last checked) / Position** — deliberately **no cost** card (unknown before a run; we don't guess). **Escalation:** the advisory (the *detail*) moved to the **left** and the command buttons stay on the **right**, in a **widened** action rail (`md:w-80`) so they're not cramped — driven by a shared `selectedIsEscalation` derivation. The shared `EscalationCommandPanel` gained one additive prop, **`showAdvisory` (default `true`** → the Pipeline Monitor is byte-unchanged); the Queue passes `false` so the panel renders command groups only and the left advisory (from the snapshot's `escalation_*` fields) isn't duplicated. The raw "Internal reason" line is **not** re-shown on the left, preserving the single-disclosure consolidation invariant (`test_ui_escalation_header_no_blame`). **Completed / failed / blocked** each get their own stat trio (Duration·Cost·Phases / Ran-for·Cost·Reached / Phases·Cost·Blocked) and a trimmed meta footer (only the timestamp that matters). The stepper, live-activity strip, and `cost_total > 0` zero-suppression are retained for the states that use them. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 6 new):* active-drops-Started/Preflight, ready-uses-nextEligible, ready-cards-not-cost, escalation-advisory-left/buttons-right, panel-showAdvisory-defaults-true, escalation-wider-rail; existing escalation/queue suites (185) + UI sweep green (same 2 pre-existing shadow failures); JSX transpile-clean. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only. *Callouts:* the queue's left advisory is a lighter presentational copy of the panel's advisory (message + suggested action), not a shared component — acceptable at this size, extract `EscalationAdvisory` if it grows; CLEAN-RATE / "Pause after phase" from the mockup still need per-phase attempt data the snapshot doesn't carry.

  **Round 3e (expansion spacing on wide screens).** The expansion's left pane carried the readable-width cap on its flex **wrapper** (`max-w-2xl`), so on wide screens the wrapper stopped growing, the ACTIONS rail stacked directly after it, and ~40% of the card was a dead void to the *right* of the rail. The cap moved to the **inner content** (`max-w-2xl`, escalation `max-w-3xl`) and the wrapper fills (`flex-1`, uncapped) — the rail now anchors the right edge of the card, matching the table rows fixed earlier the same way. Visual-only (house norm: no TDD; `test_expansion_renders_snapshot_and_action_hub` window widened for the added comment). *Changed:* `ui/index.html`.

  **Round 3d (escalation confirm-modal squash — expansion remount root cause).** Opening an escalation confirm modal and waiting ≤5s squashed/destroyed it: `QueueRowExpansion` / `QueueProjectSnapshot` / `QueueActionHub` are nested inside `QueueScreen`, so every poll-triggered render created **new component identities** — React unmounted and remounted the entire expansion subtree each tick, wiping `EscalationCommandPanel`'s open-modal state (plus the advisory disclosure, `ElapsedTimer` ticking, and any open Depends-on select; this was also the residual cause of the round-2 flash — the data guards masked it only while payloads were idle, and a concurrently running row re-renders every poll). *Fix:* the three helpers are **hook-free** (audited) and are now rendered as plain **function calls** (`QueueRowExpansion()`), keeping one render scope so module-level children (`EscalationCommandPanel`, `ElapsedTimer`, `QueueStatCard`) hold stable types and **stay mounted across polls**; the snapshot's nested `StatCard` arrow was hoisted to module-level `QueueStatCard` for the same reason; `setSnapshot` gained the same JSON identity guard as `setQueue` so idle refreshes skip the re-render entirely. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 3):* `test_expansion_subtree_called_inline_not_remounted` (forbids JSX-element renders of the three nested helpers — the regression guard), `test_expansion_renders_snapshot_and_action_hub` updated to the call form, `test_snapshot_setstate_identity_guard`. UI sweep 864 + escalation suites 72 green; JSX transpile-clean. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only.

  **Round 3c (top-bar nit).** The Progression (Auto/Manual) toggle moved to the top-LEFT of the slim top bar (`data-testid="queue-header-leading"`); the conditional Run-next / Resume-banked cluster stays right (trailing). *Tests:* `test_queue_header_trailing_wraps_progression_and_run_next` rewritten → `test_queue_header_progression_leads_run_next_trails` (asserts progression leads, run-next trails and is absent from the leading cluster). *Changed:* `ui/index.html`.

  **Round 3b (escalation rail de-crammed — operator-selected "compact rail" option).** The first escalation render stacked the Monitor-form command buttons (title + description, ~70px each) plus group labels plus the panel's own `border-2` orange frame into the 320px rail — ~700px of right rail against ~180px of advisory, leaving the left pane a void and double-framing the panel inside the expansion. Fix, chosen from three presented layouts: the shared `EscalationCommandPanel` gains a **`compact` prop (default `false`** → Monitor byte-unchanged). Compact renders **one-line buttons** — the inline description moves into the hover title (prefixed ahead of the long-form consequences), which is safe because **every command already opens a confirmation modal** restating consequences before anything fires — and swaps the outer orange frame for a frameless `space-y-3` (the rail + row pill carry the urgency). The queue passes `compact={true}`. The escalation/answered **left pane gains a Phases / Cost / Waiting stat trio** (waiting-since from `parked_at`, falling back to `waiting_for_human_at` for a live un-parked escalation; the answered variant reads "for its turn" instead of "on you") so the advisory side is informative rather than empty. Rail height drops ~60%. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 5 new):* compact-defaults-false, queue-passes-compact, desc-gated-off-in-compact, frameless-in-compact, left-waiting-stat-trio. Escalation suites (81) + UI sweep (862) green; JSX transpile-clean. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only.

  **Round 2 (operator visual review).** Four refinements after the live pass. **(1) The 5s flash:** the queue poll bumps `snapshotVersion`, and the snapshot effect flipped to a Loading state on every bump — the whole expansion visibly flashed every 5 seconds. Now stale-while-revalidate: a `snapshotIdRef` reserves the spinner (and the data clear) for a genuine target change; background refreshes of the same row swap data in place, and `fetchQueue` keeps the previous `queue`/`display_ranks` state identity when the payload is unchanged (`JSON.stringify` guard) so an idle poll re-renders nothing. **(2) Layout:** the top bar slims to the trailing Progression / Run-next / Resume cluster; the title (`text-xl`), **+ Add Project**, and Reorder controls move into a content header above the filter chips (chips lose their band styling). **(3) The table:** header and rows share one CSS-grid template (`QUEUE_TABLE_GRID`, minmax+fr columns) so columns scale proportionally on wide screens instead of PROJECT swallowing the width; row project names step up to `text-sm`. The PHASE cell no longer repeats the live agent (the STATUS pill already reads "Running executor") — parked rows keep `· {parked_agent}`. **(4) The expansion**, rebuilt as a snapshot instead of the old detail panels: no repeated name/path/status; a state headline ("Writing code", "Escalated — waiting for your answer", …, via a `STATE_HEADLINES` map — the `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` literal stays out of the snapshot body per the w4c guard), phase + goal line, a mono live-activity strip (`agent · attempt N — last action` + the ticking `ElapsedTimer`), a planner→executor→reviewer stepper, three "Run so far" stat cards (Elapsed + active-work subtext / Cost + $-per-phase / Phases + remaining), and a compact meta footer; actions sit in a fixed-width right rail under an ACTIONS eyebrow with normalized button hierarchy (primary amber **Open in Monitor** on running rows; remove = red ghost). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 3 new + 2 updated):* `test_snapshot_background_refresh_keeps_stale_data`, `test_queue_poll_preserves_identity_when_unchanged`, rewritten `test_queue_title_in_content_header_with_actions` (was `…_title_before_trailing_cluster` — asserted the old top-bar placement), `test_phase_cell_formats` now asserts the live-agent dedup. UI sweep 851 green (same 2 pre-existing shadow failures); JSX transpile-clean. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only.

- **UI REVIEW Phase 1 — chat loading indicator visibility.** The Project Ideas chat "Working on your request…" pending bubble read as a static card during an AI turn: `PendingBubbleLifeBackdrop`'s `LIVE_COLOR` and `DEAD_COLOR` were both `#1c1829` (zero-contrast — the game-of-life canvas animated invisibly) and the pending prose was `text-slate-400` at `opacity-50`. *Fix (visual, no TDD per house norm — operator reviews visually):* `DEAD_COLOR`/fade now match the bubble background (`#221b36`) and `LIVE_COLOR` is `#4a4068`, so the animation is visibly in motion; dropped the pending `opacity-50` and brightened the text to `text-slate-300`. JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only (`plans/Active/UI REVIEW/` audit, Phase 1).

- **UI REVIEW Phase 2 — live state subscription (Queue went stale; global pipeline status was screen-scoped; mid-run cost froze).** Three "the screen doesn't subscribe to live state" bugs from the Lullabeast UI audit (`plans/Active/UI REVIEW/`). **0.1** — `ctx.pipelineStatus` was written only by `PipelineScreen.fetchState`, whose interval is cleared on unmount, so the value was frozen on every other screen; `QueueScreen`'s Queue-stalled banner + Run-next gating (which read it) were stale. *Fix:* ownership moved to `App` — a new lifetime `/api/state` poll (5s, gated on `setupComplete`) is the **single writer** of `ctx.pipelineStatus`; `PipelineScreen` no longer writes it (keeps its local `pState` for detail rendering). `QueueScreen` still reads `ctx.pipelineStatus` and still does **not** fetch `/api/state` itself — its per-entry advisory stays snapshot-sourced (the G3 architecture rule, guarded by `test_ui_queue_escalation_uses_shared_panel.py`). **0.2** — `QueueScreen` fetched only on mount + after mutations, so it didn't reflect orchestrator-driven advance (ACTIVE→COMPLETED, dependency clears, escalation, per-entry progress). *Fix:* a 5s `/api/queue` poll that skips entirely while a reorder is in flight (`reorderModeRef` guard) so it can't clobber a drag or spam the reorder-dirty error. **0.3** — `fetchMetricsSummary` was one-shot on mount, freezing the "Run so far" cost mid-run. *Fix:* added to the `PipelineScreen` interval (5s). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 5):* new `tests/test_ui_phase2_live_state_subscription.py` — App owns the live status poll / PipelineScreen no longer writes it / Queue polls / Queue poll skips during reorder / metrics polled. 5 new + 119 regression (queue/pipeline/app/activity-feed/log static contracts) green; JSX transpile-clean. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only (App status poll added; PipelineScreen `setPipelineStatus` write removed + `fetchMetricsSummary` added to the interval; QueueScreen poll added). *Callouts (not actioned):* redundant `/api/state` poll on the Pipeline screen (App status poll + PipelineScreen full-state poll) — eliminating it needs lifting full state into context (a large refactor, out of scope); no JSON-equality guard on the Queue poll (re-renders every 5s, consistent with `PipelineScreen.fetchState`).

- **UI REVIEW Phase 3 — Setup & onboarding flow (dead-ends and misleading errors at project start).** Five project-start bugs from the Lullabeast UI audit (`plans/Active/UI REVIEW/`). **N1 — thin/abort-stub roadmap dead-end.** A low-readiness PRD converts to a roadmap with no parseable phases; **Continue to Setup** then threw the misleading *"Roadmap Format Invalid — At least one valid phase line is required"* with a useless **Fix Format** CTA (root cause is an under-developed PRD, not a format error). *Fix:* `_validate_roadmap_content` now tags the zero-phase short-circuit error with a machine-readable `code: "no_phase_lines"` (additive); the Ideas screen routes that case (and the existing `"Transformation Aborted"` literal) to a new **soft** "Your PRD needs more detail" modal (`data-testid="ideas-roadmap-needs-detail"`) — **Keep editing PRD / Regenerate / Continue anyway →**. The **Continue anyway** bypass (`_proceedToPreflight` → `navigateToPreflightWithSeed`, skipping validation) keeps the gate *advisory* so a deliberately small post-MVP enhancement roadmap (down to one phase) is never trapped — an operator-requested refinement to the audit's plan. The `showFormatInvalidModal` + **Fix Format** path is **kept** for malformed-but-substantive roadmaps (phases present, missing `> Test:`/Behavioral Verification). **N2 — sticky Add-Project path.** The path field kept the previous value on reopen (browser autofill + recents `<datalist>`). *Fix:* `autoComplete="off"` (+ autoCorrect/autoCapitalize/spellCheck) on the shared `ServerPathInput`, and the `<datalist>` is removed. **N4 — existing-repo launch gap.** Setup is Ideas-only, so an existing repo with `roadmap.md` on disk but no linked idea couldn't be launched from Setup, with no guidance. *Fix:* confirming such a repo does a real `POST /api/setup/repo-roadmap-hint` (the dead P0-Stage-J.4 removal comment is deleted) and, when `found && !hasLinkedIdea`, pops a redirect modal (`data-testid="setup-repo-needs-idea"`) whose CTA jumps to the Queue and opens **+ Add Project prefilled** with the staged path — via a new `pendingQueueAddPath` app-context channel → `QueueScreen` effect → `AddProjectModal initialPath`. **4-A/4-B — hidden recents.** Recents surfaced only as an autocomplete `<datalist>` and could include `/tmp`/dead paths. *Fix:* `GET /api/setup/recent-projects` now returns only real on-disk dirs (`_live_recent_projects`); `ServerPathInput` renders a styled, app-themed recents **dropdown** (opens on focus, type-to-filter, click-to-select; rounded/dark-purple to match the app — not the bare native OS autocomplete) shared by all three repository selectors (Setup / Add-Project / Switch-Project), fed by `recentsToDisplayPaths` (`/tmp` filtered as a display backstop). **No user-facing delete** (the DELETE/prune endpoint stays test-only); a new session-scoped `tests/conftest.py` teardown calls `POST …/prune` so suite runs self-clean. **4-C — banner/launch predicate divergence.** The preflight "currently running" banner used `queueEntriesHaveBusyLivePipeline` (live status) while Launch keys on `state === 'ACTIVE'`. *Fix:* a new `queueHasActiveEntry` helper feeds the banner from the launch predicate; `queueEntriesHaveBusyLivePipeline` is **retained** (still gates first-run Ideas routing via `shouldOpenIdeasOnColdBootstrap`). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 25 new):* `tests/test_ui_phase3_setup_onboarding.py` (17 static contracts across N1/N2/N4/4-A/4-C) + extensions to `test_api_setup_validate_roadmap.py` (`no_phase_lines` code), `test_api_setup_recent_projects.py` (GET real-dir filter + conftest-teardown contract), `test_ui_queue_add_modal.py` (`initialPath` prefill). **Replaced stale-contract tests** (removal = addition): `test_ui_server_path_input.py::test_has_datalist_element` and `test_browser_path_selector.py`'s two `*_datalist_link` tests + `test_recents_appear_after_preflight` (the datalist DOM is gone; the E2E recents test now uses a non-`/tmp` dir so it survives the new display filter), and `test_ui_preflight_screen.py`'s `test_preflight_confirm_path_fetches_repo_roadmap_hint` (was passing against the *removal comment*) + the `TestAppPreflightQueueActiveGating` "B-02" class (asserted the inverse of 4-C — the older decision the roadmap deliberately supersedes). Full UI suite green except 2 pre-existing `test_defensive_c3_05` failures (fail on HEAD, unrelated) and 2 known ideas/convert full-run flakes (pass in isolation). JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/index.html`, `ui/server.py`, `tests/conftest.py` (+ the test files above). *Callouts (not actioned):* **4-D** (remove the unused `seedRoadmap` prop from `PreflightScreen`) is a Phase 6 item, left for that pass; **Continue anyway** on a genuinely zero-phase roadmap can seed a non-runnable roadmap (preflight/`phase_resolver` then find nothing) — intended, operator-agency by design.

- **UI REVIEW Phase 4 — cost & token visibility (Pipeline Monitor).** Five spend-surface defects from the Lullabeast UI audit (`plans/Active/UI REVIEW/`); the three open "OR" decisions were taken with the architect (wire `run_started_at` / per-phase tokens / render the orphan role cost). **3-A — dead "(from previous run)" staleness badge.** `RoadmapPanel` bound `runStartedAt` to `pState.run_started_at`, but the field had **zero Python writers** and was absent from `/api/state`, so the badge could never render (an intended-but-half-built "W5-D" feature; a test even asserted its presence). *Fix (wired, not removed):* `run_started_at` is now a run-scoped `pipeline_state.json` field (ISO8601) — stamped at every fresh-run write (server `_clean_pipeline_state_for_project` → launch/switch-project; orchestrator constructor, queue auto-advance, and CLI project-switch dicts), **preserved across phase advance** (the advance mutates state in place) **and across park→revival** (added to the park snapshot + both revival-restore branches), and exposed by `GET /api/state` (an explicit whitelist — added deliberately). Resume paths (git-recover/resume-ready) read-modify-write existing state, so they preserve it. **3-B — tokens produced but never surfaced.** Each `metrics.jsonl` row already carries per-role `total_tokens`, but `/api/metrics-summary` returned only cost. *Fix:* a `_role_token_total` helper (parallel to `_role_cost`) adds run-level `total_tokens` + per-role `{planner,executor,reviewer}_tokens_total` and per-phase `tokens_total` (additive; missing/non-numeric → 0). Rendered in the RoadmapPanel "Run so far" line, a completion **Total Tokens** card (per-role breakdown), and a per-phase **Tokens** column (gated like cost). **3-C — orphan per-phase role cost.** `/api/metrics-summary` already returned per-phase `planner_cost`/`executor_cost`/`reviewer_cost` with no UI consumer. *Fix (rendered, not dropped):* the RoadmapPanel expanded phase detail now shows the per-role cost split. **3-D — cost frozen mid-run.** Already plumbed by Phase 2's `@5s` `fetchMetricsSummary` interval; the new cost/token figures read from the polled `metricsSummary`, so they update live. **3-E — `COMMAND_LABELS` gap.** Added `PROCEED` ("Mark Complete") and `NUCLEAR_RESET` ("Reset Everything & Restart Phase"), reused verbatim from `ESCALATION_CMD_DEFS`. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 18 new across 4 files):* `tests/test_api_phase4_metrics_tokens.py` (token aggregation + default-0 + cost-unchanged guard), `tests/test_api_phase4_run_started_at.py` (`/api/state` exposure + null-when-absent + reset-template stamp), `autodev/tests/test_run_started_at_lifecycle.py` (behavioural survives-phase-advance + source-wiring for the stamp/snapshot/restore sites), `tests/test_ui_phase4_cost_token_visibility.py` (Run-so-far tokens / completion Total-Tokens + per-phase column / per-phase role cost / COMMAND_LABELS / metrics-poll guard). Also extended `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_queue.py` — the park-snapshot drift-guard (`_SNAPSHOT_KEYS`) and the behavioural revival-restore test now cover `run_started_at` (capture + restore). 264 targeted tests green (new + kept-green cost/Stage-H/completion-report/Phase-2 guards + an orchestrator regression sweep); JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/server.py` (metrics-summary tokens, `/api/state` field, reset-template stamp), `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` (6 stamp/preserve sites), `ui/index.html` (`fmtTokens` helper, Run-so-far + completion + expanded-detail renders, `COMMAND_LABELS`). *Follow-up (live MCP-browser validation, 2026-06-09):* the live pass found `run_started_at` was **null on queue-launched runs** — `POST /api/queue/trigger-next` spawns the orchestrator with `--project-path`, and re-running a finished project hit `apply_cli_project_path`'s *same-project* `else` branch, which loads disk state and never stamped (the original Phase-4 orchestrator sites covered the project-*switch* branch but not a same-project re-start). *Fix:* the same-project branch now stamps a fresh `run_started_at` **unless** the on-disk status is in-flight (`_RESUMABLE_ACTIVE_RUN_STATUSES = RUNNING/WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL/WAITING_FOR_HUMAN/QUEUE_HALTED` → a crash-resume, where the original stamp is preserved). +2 TDD tests in `autodev/tests/test_run_started_at_lifecycle.py` (fresh-restart stamps / active-resume preserves), confirmed live (`trigger-next` → `WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL` with a non-null `run_started_at`). The same pass also **corrected the badge's verification expectation**: the "(from previous run)" badge renders at `PIPELINE_COMPLETE` (gated, `ui/index.html:2917`), *not* mid-run — it flags a completion report that predates the current run (e.g. a `completion_review:false` run over an older report), so it is correctly hidden whenever the run regenerates a fresh report. *Callouts (not actioned):* a bare external `IDLE` reset (dev/tooling, not the Setup-launch flow) does not stamp `run_started_at`, so the badge stays hidden until a real run writes it — graceful degradation by design; the per-phase **per-role token** split is intentionally not surfaced (only per-phase total tokens + per-role cost) to match scope and avoid an int-vs-dict key collision with the on-disk row's `planner_tokens` dict.

- **UI REVIEW Phase 5 — parent/child dependency visibility + doc correction (R1).** A child whose parent is not yet `COMPLETED` is genuinely held (the orchestrator's `_select_next_queue_project` skips any child whose parent ≠ COMPLETED), but the queue **state** only becomes `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` when the parent is in a *blocking* state (`parent_blocks_child()` → `{BLOCKED, ESCALATION, ESCALATION_ANSWERED}`). For a `READY`/`ACTIVE` parent the child stays `READY`, so the wait was **invisible**: the row showed a plain "READY" pill (looked startable) and the "Waiting for:" detail panel was hard-gated on the literal `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` state. Live MCP-browser validation caught this (audit finding R1; the backend was confirmed *correct* — the bug was purely UI + docs). *Fix (UI-only, decision R1 = B — keep the selection-skip semantics, surface the wait):* one shared `isWaitingForParent(parentEntry)` helper (`parent.state !== 'COMPLETED'`, mirroring the orchestrator skip) now drives a **"Waiting for: <parent>"** indicator for **any** incomplete parent across three surfaces — a muted queue-row sub-line (suppressed on `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` rows, which already show the orange "Waiting on parent" pill), the project-snapshot line (re-gated off the literal state; the dead `(parent not found)` fallback removed), and the action-hub panel (extracted to a single `waitingForParentPanel` const rendered in **both** the `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` and `READY`/`SKIPPED_PENDING` branches instead of forked into one). No state-machine or server change — the backend already exposes every entry's `parent_id`/`state` via `GET /api/queue` (proven by `tests/test_queue_dependency_edit.py`). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 5 new + 2 rewritten):* new `tests/test_ui_phase5_waiting_for_parent.py` (helper defined / COMPLETED-excluded / shared panel in both branches / panel gated on the helper / row sub-line); in `tests/test_w4c_dependency_hold_parent_name.py`, `test_dependency_hold_guard` → `test_waiting_for_gated_on_incomplete_parent` (asserts the helper gate and that `selected.state === 'DEPENDENCY_HOLD'` is **gone** from the snapshot) and `test_graceful_missing_parent` → `test_no_dead_missing_parent_fallback` (the dead `(parent not found)` branch is removed). 10 Phase-5 + 828 static-UI regression tests green; JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` only. *Docs:* `CLAUDE.md` ("DEPENDENCY_HOLD state" — the stale "non-COMPLETED parent → DEPENDENCY_HOLD" claim corrected to the blocking-state rule + a UI-surfacing note) and `GLOSSARY.md` (the `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` row's stale "Parent project not `COMPLETED`" cause). *Callouts (not actioned):* `depBadgeTitle` still encodes the `!== 'COMPLETED'` predicate inline (it needs 3-way wording, so it can't reduce to the boolean) — minor duplication; the action-hub `DEPENDENCY_HOLD` vs `READY` branches stay separate (merging is beyond R1); the row `aria-label` still reads the pill label ("READY") for a waiting child (screen-reader enrichment is out of scope for this visibility pass).

- **UI REVIEW Phase 6 — orphan endpoints & cleanup (the audit's final, low-visibility pass).** Two code fixes + two documentation-only items from the Lullabeast UI audit (`plans/Active/UI REVIEW/`); no state-machine, orchestrator, or API-behaviour change. **1-H — copy buttons silent on a blocked clipboard.** The PRD-All / Roadmap / Verification document-footer copy buttons called `navigator.clipboard.writeText(...)` raw — no fallback, no `.catch`, no user feedback — so a browser that blocks clipboard access failed silently. *Fix:* one shared module-level `copyToClipboard(text)` helper (async Clipboard API → `execCommand` textarea fallback, returns a success boolean) is now the single source of truth; the three buttons dispatch through a new `handleDocCopy(tab, text)` and show `Copied!` / `Copy failed` for ~2s via a `docCopyState` + `docCopyLabel` in `IdeasScreen`. The existing per-section "⎘" button — which carried its own *duplicated* inline clipboard→textarea fallback — was **migrated onto the same helper** (DRY; its success-only UX preserved). **4-D — dead `seedRoadmap` prop.** `seedRoadmap` was destructured in `PreflightScreen` but never read — a redundant shadow of the live `roadmapSeed` (both were always set to the same value). *Fix:* the whole 6-site chain removed (destructure + `useState` + the two setters + the `appCtxValue` export + the prop pass); every `roadmapSeed` site is untouched. **1-C — `triggerDraftingIndicator` is post-hoc (decision: keep, document).** Added a code comment recording that the "✏ Drafting…" banner fires only *after* a reply returns and auto-clears after 3s — a true during-turn signal needs streaming infra OpenClaw lacks (opaque model calls); the "Working…" pending bubble is the live signal. **1-D — `clarity-check` endpoint parked (decision: keep backend-only, no UI).** Added a docstring note to `POST /api/ideas/{id}/clarity-check` marking it intentionally unwired (the deprecated PRD clarity/completeness review; siblings `alignment_report`/`adversarial_report` likewise parked) so future audits don't re-flag it as a dead endpoint. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 7 new):* `tests/test_ui_phase6_cleanup.py` — helper-exists-with-`execCommand`-fallback-and-`catch` / no-naked-`writeText(prdContent|roadmapContent|verificationContent)` / buttons-use-`handleDocCopy` / `Copied!`+`Copy failed` present / per-section-migrated-to-helper / `seedRoadmap`-fully-removed (case-insensitive) / `roadmapSeed`-still-present guard. **Replaced stale-contract tests** (removal = addition, per the house norm): in `tests/test_ui_preflight_screen.py`, dropped `'seedRoadmap'` from `test_preflight_accepts_all_required_props`' prop list (it asserted the now-removed dead prop — a passing test for dead code) and reworded the misleading `test_pre_populated_roadmap_shows_indicator` docstring (it credited `seedRoadmap` for an indicator actually driven by the linked-idea summary card). 797 frontend static-contract tests green (incl. the 7 new + the untouched completion-report clipboard guard + `test_api_ideas_clarity_check`); JSX transpile-clean via `ui/static/babel.min.js`. *Changed:* `ui/index.html` (the `copyToClipboard` helper, the `IdeasScreen` feedback state/handlers, 3 button rewires, the per-section migration, 6 `seedRoadmap` deletions, the 1-C comment) and `ui/server.py` (the 1-D docstring note). *Callouts (not actioned):* the completion-report code-block copy button (`ui/index.html`, the markdown-renderer event-delegation path) still uses a `try/catch` + `textContent` swap with **no** fallback — a different subsystem, out of Phase 6 scope; it keeps `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, so its `test_ui_pipeline_completion_report` guard stays green. The per-section "⎘" stays success-only (no `Copy failed`) — the failure surface was scoped to the three doc buttons by decision. `CLAUDE.md`/`GLOSSARY.md` left unchanged — Phase 6 changes no architectural behaviour (the clarity-check idle-detection mention stays accurate).

- **Issue #2 (Minecraft) — queue-entry parked-metadata hygiene (Defect C) + symmetric, state-aware symlink writes (Defect A).** Two control-plane defects that corrupted state when a project was paused/switched mid-escalation. **Defect C:** the server's `_queue_mark_matching_entry_active` (promote + demote) and `_queue_demote_stale_active_entries` moved rows to ACTIVE/READY **without scrubbing the `parked_*` fields**, and the orchestrator's `_queue_restore_parked_entry_to_active` scrubbed only 3 of 5 — so a parked row could surface as `state=READY` while still carrying a stale `parked_state_snapshot` (observed live: `Minecraft` READY + a CORE-E1 snapshot + `started_at=null` after it was parked → switch-projected to ACTIVE → displaced when `baseball` took over). *Fix:* one canonical scrub — `queue_semantics.scrub_parked_fields(entry)` over `PARKED_ENTRY_FIELDS = {parked_state_snapshot, parked_at, parked_reason, parked_pipeline_status, answered_at}`, shared by the orchestrator (2 selection activations + restore) and the server (both reconcile helpers), applied on every non-revival transition. **And resume/switch now route a *parked* (`ESCALATION`/`ESCALATION_ANSWERED`) target through the existing `--revive` path** (`_spawn_orchestrator(revive_entry_id=…)` → `apply_cli_revive` → `_select_next_queue_project(target_entry_id=…)`), skipping the phase-0 state write + bare promote (mirroring `POST /api/queue/{id}/relaunch`), so the escalated phase + any banked command are *restored* instead of restarting. **Defect A:** `POST /api/setup/preflight` repointed the LIVE symlink as a side effect, guarded only against a live orchestrator on a *different* project — so an idle-time preview of project X hijacked the link while `pipeline_state` targeted Y (the live r&mpop↔Minecraft divergence); and server-side symlink writes moved only the AUTODEV-side link, leaving the agent-followed OpenClaw-side link stale. *Fix:* (1) all three server repoint sites (`_repoint_pipeline_project_symlink`, `_run_preflight_checks`, `_run_init_project`) now write **both** links transactionally via new `_atomic_symlink_swap_multi` + `_pipeline_symlink_paths` (the OpenClaw link is included only when `openclaw_root` is configured — always so in production, so an under-specified hermetic test falls back to single-link and can't clobber the operator's real link); (2) the preflight guard is now **state-aware** — it refuses to repoint *away from* `pipeline_state.project_path` regardless of orchestrator liveness. *Tests (TDD, red→green):* new `autodev/tests/test_queue_semantics_scrub.py`, `autodev/tests/test_queue_active_reconcile_scrub.py`, `tests/test_queue_revival_routing.py`, `tests/test_preflight_symmetric_symlink.py`; extended `tests/test_queue_active_reconcile.py` (+3) and `tests/test_preflight_symlink_guard.py` (+2 state-aware); launch's single-swap assertions updated to per-link symmetric. New autouse `tests/conftest.py::_protect_pipeline_symlinks` snapshots+restores the live `pipeline-project` links around every test (operationalising the documented "tests rewrite the shared symlink" hazard, widened by the symmetric write). 186 targeted tests green, live links intact. *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/queue_semantics.py`, `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `ui/server.py`, `tests/conftest.py`. *Defect B (stray `pipeline-project/` litter dirs) is a separate pass.*

- **Phase 9 — abort the in-flight agent session on escalation (zombie-session guard; resolves Phase 8 callout #1).** When the orchestrator gave up on a phase and routed to escalation, it left the last-invoked pipeline agent's OpenClaw session **streaming**. The executor retry-start abort (`orchestrator.py`, `if retries > 0`) only stops the *prior* attempt when *launching the next* — so the **terminal** attempt that triggers escalation was never aborted. That zombie (especially the executor, which has `exec`) could keep running `git commit` / `git tag` / file edits **after** the orchestrator handed off to the human, corrupting repo state. Observed live (2026-06-06, Tick-Tac-Toe): a leftover executor session committed `CORE-E1` and created a git tag literally named `phase_base_commit` ~64 s *after* escalation (the tag is inert — the executor gate reads `phase_base_commit` from `pipeline_state.json`, not a tag — but the stray commit + tag left confusing state and could race the orchestrator's own git ops at recovery). *Confirmed OpenClaw capability:* `openclaw@2026.5.18` exposes only cooperative `sessions.abort` / `sessions.cancel` (`AbortController`, keyed by `runId`) — there is **no force-kill** of a single in-flight run short of killing the whole gateway. So the fix sends the abort the orchestrator previously omitted at escalation, plus a fallback "halt — end your turn" message. *Fix:* a new `_record_active_agent(role, session_key)` (called at all 3 agent invocation sites — planner/executor/reviewer) tracks the in-flight session; `_abort_active_agent_session("escalation")` (called **once** in the escalation dispatch first-invocation block, guarded by `_should_invoke_escalation_agent()` flipping RUNNING→WAITING_FOR_HUMAN) aborts it via the existing `abort_agent_session` + `verify_session_stopped`. It injects `_HALT_SESSION_MESSAGE` (a new module constant) via `invoke_agent_webhook` **only when the session is NOT confirmed-stopped** (abort failed, or acked-but-stamp-still-advancing) — **not** when confirmed-stopped, since waking a stopped session just to read "do nothing" is wasted work (operator-directed). Best-effort + soft-continue: a webhook failure is logged not raised, and escalation is never blocked. **Generic across all agents** (operator-confirmed scope). *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` only — module constant `_HALT_SESSION_MESSAGE`; 3 process-local fields in `__init__` (`_active_agent_session_key`/`_active_agent_role`/`_active_agent_stamp`); methods `_record_active_agent` + `_abort_active_agent_session`; 3 record calls at the planner/executor/reviewer invocations; 1 abort call at the escalation dispatch. The existing retry-start abort is **unchanged** (independent infra). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 9):* new `autodev/tests/test_escalation_aborts_inflight_session.py` — confirmed-stopped does NOT inject halt (the key operator-directed assertion) / verify-failed injects halt + emits `abort_verify_failed` / abort-failed injects halt (skips verify) / no-op when nothing in-flight / halt-message best-effort (raising webhook doesn't propagate) / `_record_active_agent` sets fields + 3 structural pins (helpers defined, dispatch aborts, 3 sites record). Pipeline suite **1207 passed, 0 regressions**. *Docs:* `CLAUDE.md` (abort-on-escalation narrative + `abort_attempted`/`abort_verify_failed` now carry `source="escalation"` + `halt_message_sent`), `plans/Active/defensive-hardening-roadmap.md` (Phase 9). *Callouts (not actioned):* cooperative-abort latency means a zombie can still write in the now-bounded window (OpenClaw limitation); the inert `phase_base_commit` tag isn't specially cleaned (`RESET_PHASE`/`NUCLEAR_RESET` already wipe zombie commits via branch reset); re-abort at recovery-command-consume time, halt-message on the retry-start path, and `phase_state`-durable session-key (restart-into-escalation) are all deferred.

- **Phase 8 — queue auto-advance startup parity (in-process advance skipped phase-0 init → `ERR_MISSING_BASE_COMMIT`).** When the queue advanced **in-process** to the next project — a project completing (`PIPELINE_COMPLETE`), a roadmap `[!]` block, or an escalation-park, all under `queue_mode="auto"` — the orchestrator re-entered the **main loop** directly instead of re-running the startup phase-0 sequence. `_select_next_queue_project` writes a blank fresh-start state (`current_phase:0, current_phase_raw_id:"", current_agent:"planner"`), so the planner ran at `Phase=NONE`/empty `raw_id`, the branch was created as `phase/0`, and **`phase_base_commit` was never captured** → the executor gate's MiniMax-deletion guard failed closed with `ERR_MISSING_BASE_COMMIT` on every attempt → blame-cap → escalation. The phase-0 init (resolve `phase 0 → first real phase`, capture `phase_base_commit`, checkout `phase/<raw_id>`) lived only in `_run_startup_planner_phase_zero_and_branch`, invoked **once** by `run()` before the loop; the startup path's *own* `PIPELINE_COMPLETE` advance already re-ran it via the `"retry_startup"` signal, but the three main-loop advance sites returned `"continue"`/`continue` with no re-init — the asymmetry that was the bug. Observed live on the `checkers2 → Tick-Tac-Toe` auto-advance (2026-06-06) — the **first true in-process advance** in the orchestrator log; every prior switch went through the fresh-launch "Project switch detected" path, which *does* init, which is why it had never surfaced. *Fix:* extract `run()`'s inline startup loop into `Orchestrator._run_startup_loop()` and call it after **all three** in-process advances — the `PIPELINE_COMPLETE` arm and the BLOCKED park-advance arm in `_advance_to_next_pending_phase`, and the escalation-park advance in `run()`'s main loop — so a queued project is resolved + branched + base-committed exactly as a fresh launch is. `_run_startup_loop` is a safe no-op on a **revival** activation (`current_agent="escalation"`, on which the startup fn early-returns), so the Stage-H restore path is untouched. **No new pipeline state; no behavior change** for fresh launches (the loop was *moved*, not altered) or revivals. *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` only — new `_run_startup_loop()` (extracted from `run()`); `run()` delegates; `_advance_to_next_pending_phase` PIPELINE_COMPLETE + BLOCKED arms re-init (`exit_run → "break"`, else `"continue"`); main-loop escalation-park advance re-inits (`exit_run → break`, else `continue`); docstrings on `_run_startup_loop`, `_advance_to_next_pending_phase`, `_run_startup_planner_phase_zero_and_branch`. *Tests (TDD, red→green — 11):* new `autodev/tests/test_queue_advance_startup_parity.py` (4 `_run_startup_loop` loop-semantics unit tests — settle / retry-then-settle / exit_run / 20-pass cap — + 4 source-grep structural pins: method defined, `run()` delegates & drops the inline `while _startup_pass < 20`, both advance-helper arms re-init, every `_queue_after_park_maybe_advance()` site triggers a re-init); `test_phase_advance_helper.py` — the existing `test_advance_pipeline_complete_auto_advances_returns_continue` **strengthened + renamed** `…_reinits_and_returns_continue` (it asserted only `"continue"` — a passing test for the half-built contract — now also asserts the re-init runs), + new `test_advance_complete_auto_advance_exit_run_returns_break` and `test_advance_blocked_auto_advance_runs_startup_loop`. Pipeline suite **1198 passed, 0 regressions**. *Docs:* `CLAUDE.md` (Queue System — in-process-advance re-init note), `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` (advance-helper queue-advance re-init), `plans/Active/defensive-hardening-roadmap.md` (Phase 8). *Callouts (not actioned):* a leftover executor session committed `Tick-Tac-Toe`'s `CORE-E1` + an **inert** git tag named `phase_base_commit` ~64 s **after** escalation — the documented `abort_verify_failed` soft-continue letting a zombie session mutate the repo post-escalation (the gate reads the JSON field, not a tag); flagged for a separate look. Site #3 (escalation-park) has no isolated behavioral test — main-loop integration is heavy; covered by the structural pin.

- **Phase 7 — documentation & cross-OS correctness sweep (T7.1–T7.4).** Closed the doc-vs-code drift left by Phases 1–6 and the one real cross-OS exposure under the confirmed **Linux / macOS / WSL2** matrix (native Windows out). TDD red→green: 3 new visual-boundary tests in `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_gate_visual_verification.py` + 13 static-lint assertions in `tests/test_defensive_p7_docs_and_crossos.py`, all RED against the pre-change tree and GREEN after.

  - **T7.4 — reviewer-gate visual-path boundary closed (operator-confirmed scope expansion).** `_check_visual_verification` validated `visual_smoke_artifacts` paths with only `isabs`+`exists`, so an in-workspace symlink (or an absolute path) resolving *outside* the workspace was silently accepted — the asymmetry flagged as a deferred T1.4 callout (behavioral/regression evidence already had the check). It now applies the identical `os.path.realpath` + `os.path.commonpath` boundary check (rejects escapes, `ValueError`-safe), byte-for-byte the pattern T1.4 shipped 4× in the same/sibling gate. The operator chose to **close the gap in code** rather than only document it.
  - **T7.2 — POSIX/WSL2 runtime guard + `os.sep` hygiene (Decision #3).** The three `fcntl`-importing entry points (`orchestrator.py`, `heartbeat_cron.py`, `ui/server.py`) wrap `import fcntl` in `try/except ModuleNotFoundError` → `raise SystemExit("…requires Linux, macOS, or WSL2 — run under WSL2")`, so native-Windows Python fails friendly instead of a raw `ModuleNotFoundError` (mirrors `install.sh:73`'s install-time rejection; the guard is transparent on POSIX, where `fcntl` imports normally). The three `os.path.basename(<project>.rstrip("/"))` project-name derivations in `server.py` now strip with `os.sep` (T1.5 idiom; a no-op under POSIX but the correct cross-OS form).
  - **T7.1 — `_spawn_orchestrator` doc drift.** CLAUDE.md's "Config Dual-Source Rule" still called the spawn-path construction "a known unfixed issue" (with the old bare-filename form) and still claimed `autodev_repo_path` falls back to `~/.openclaw`. Both corrected to match the code (`server.py:471` → repo-root `_AUTODEV_UI_ROOT`; `server.py:1666-1667` → `os.path.join(autodev_repo_path, "autodev", "pipeline", ORCHESTRATOR_FILENAME)`). Unresolved Items #1/#3 were already correct and remain the resolved-issues record.
  - **T7.3 — CAS-closure purity documented at the call sites.** A one-line `CAS-pure` marker now sits above all 23 `_mutate_queue` (orchestrator ×9) / `_mutate_queue_file` (server ×14) call sites, signposting the load-bearing invariant (no spawn/symlink/IO inside the retried closure; re-applied ≤`QUEUE_MAX_CAS_RETRIES`×). A lint test enforces the marker on every future call site; `queue_semantics.mutate_queue`'s docstring + the T5.5 `RuntimeError` enforcement (version-key mutation) remain authoritative.

  - **Cross-OS audit — triaged (the T7.2 "findings triaged" verification deliverable):**

    | Finding | OS exposure | Disposition |
    |---|---|---|
    | `import fcntl` unguarded ×3 | native Windows: raw `ModuleNotFoundError` | **fix-now** — friendly `SystemExit` guard |
    | `os.path.basename(*.rstrip("/"))` ×3 (`server.py`) | latent (native Windows only) | **fix-now** — `os.sep` (free, T1.5 idiom) |
    | `/tmp/orchestrator.log`, `/tmp/ui-server.log` | none — POSIX `/tmp` valid on Linux/macOS/WSL2 | **leave** — `ORCHESTRATOR_SPAWN_LOG_PATH` is patched/asserted across ~12 tests; migrating to `tempfile.gettempdir()` is churn+risk for zero benefit |
    | `install.sh` embedded-Python `.rstrip("/")` + `f"{root}/…"` | none — bash script never runs native-Windows | **leave** — not a cross-OS bug |
    | `fcntl.flock` advisory lock | POSIX-only by design (Decision #3) | **keep** — guard makes its absence friendly |
    | gate-script `os.sep`, `expanduser`/`$HOME`, URL `.rstrip("/")` | none | **already correct** — no action |

  *Changed:* `reviewer_gate.py` (visual boundary + docstring); `orchestrator.py` / `heartbeat_cron.py` / `ui/server.py` (fcntl guard; server +3 `os.sep` +14 markers; orchestrator +9 markers). *Tests (TDD — 16 new):* `test_reviewer_gate_visual_verification.py` (+3 boundary), `tests/test_defensive_p7_docs_and_crossos.py` (+13 static-lint). *Docs:* CLAUDE.md (spawn-path ×2 sites, POSIX/WSL2 platform note, reviewer-gate security bullet); SETUP.md/README already documented the platform matrix correctly (untouched, no duplication). *No behavioral test for the `fcntl` except-branch or the `os.sep` swap* — both are unreachable / a runtime no-op on POSIX CI, so they are covered structurally by the guard-presence + `os.sep` lints (the established `test_docs_no_false_linux_only.py` idiom).

- **Phase 6 concurrency & lifecycle integrity (T6.1–T6.7).** The defensive-hardening roadmap's highest-blast-radius tranche: a single root cause — the orchestrator acquired `pipeline.lock` only *after* `apply_cli_*` had already rewritten `pipeline_state.json` — made the server's lock-based liveness guard a wide TOCTOU window, so a second spawn could rewind an in-flight pipeline to phase 0 before dying; plus three unguarded endpoints, two spawn-then-write-state setup endpoints, a non-crash-idempotent reviewer-PASS merge→advance window, a non-transactional `update_symlink`, and a malformed-queue-row crash that poisoned selection for every project. Landed in four reviewed-by-group slices, all design decisions operator-locked (Decision #1 lock-first; Decision #2 durable `phase_merged` marker). TDD red→green (40 new tests across three `test_defensive_p6_*.py` files + `tests/test_defensive_p6_server_liveness.py`); pipeline suite **1185 passed, 0 regressions**. The reviewer-PASS / lock designs were independently stress-tested before coding; several validated refinements below correct the roadmap's first-pass framing.

  - **T6.7 — defensive queue-file parsing (land-first quick win).** A valid-JSON wrong-shape `pipeline_queue.json` (`{}`, `[]`, or a dict with no `queue` list) was returned by `_read_queue` as-is → `queue_data["queue"]` `KeyError` on **every** restart; and unlike corrupt JSON it was never quarantined, so the bad file was re-read and re-crashed each cycle (a heartbeat-cron restart loop). `_read_queue` now shape-validates after `json.load` and routes a wrong-shape file through the **same** quarantine-and-raise path as corrupt JSON (extracted into a shared `_quarantine_queue_file` helper), so the bad file is renamed away and the next read self-heals to the empty structure. Separately, the selection walk sanitizes its in-memory entry list (skip + log any row missing a usable `id` / `state` / `project_path`) and tolerates a missing `position` in the sort; the find-by-id CAS generators, `_skip_and_requeue_group`, `_get_all_descendants` (truthy-id guard — a `None` id must not match a `None` parent_id) and `_move_group_atomically` were made subscript-tolerant so one malformed on-disk row can't `KeyError` the CAS re-apply path. Behavior unchanged for well-formed input.
  - **T6.6 — CAS exhaustion in the selection path degrades to retry-next-cycle.** `_select_next_queue_project` and `_promote_answered_escalations` called `_mutate_queue` unwrapped, so a `QueueVersionConflict` (perpetual queue contention, after the 8 retries in `mutate_queue`) propagated to `run()`'s top-level handler → escalation. The selection walk is now a thin `_select_next_queue_project` wrapper that catches `QueueVersionConflict` → logs → returns `False` ("couldn't commit this cycle — retry next cycle") around `_select_next_queue_project_inner`; `_promote_answered_escalations` catches its own (it has callers beyond selection — the `_maybe_revive_on_queue_halted` recovery hook).
  - **T6.1 — lock before state write + transactional symlink + spawn hardening (Decision #1).** `acquire_lock()` is now **idempotent** (returns immediately when `self.lock_fd` is set) so it can be lifted into `main()` ahead of the `apply_cli_*` calls while `run()` still calls it first; a losing second orchestrator started during the boot window `sys.exit(1)`s **before** mutating shared state. **Refinement (validation):** idempotency is load-bearing, not cosmetic — fcntl locks bind to the *open file description*, so a second `os.open`+`flock` from the same process is denied (`BlockingIOError`→exit); without the guard the lift would break every run (pinned by a pure-stdlib regression test). `update_symlink` is now **transactional** — both project symlinks are staged at unique temp names and committed with atomic `os.replace`; a second-link failure rolls the first back to its prior target so the orchestrator/agent trees are never left permanently divergent (the old two-`ln -sfn` form had no rollback). Server-side, `_spawn_orchestrator` wraps `subprocess.Popen` in `try/except OSError` (no uncaught 500), closes the parent's log fd after Popen (was leaking one fd per spawn), and gained an opt-in `confirm_lock` that polls `_check_orchestrator_liveness` post-spawn (`_SPAWN_LOCK_CONFIRM_TIMEOUT` 10 s) to confirm the child took the lock before reporting success. **Refinement (validation B1):** `confirm_lock` defaults **False** and is wired only into the four interactive endpoints (launch / switch / resume / recover) — never the fire-and-forget autostart spawn (`_queue_run_trigger_next_logic`), where a slow-but-healthy child would otherwise get its ACTIVE row marked FAILED + a 500 on `POST /api/queue/add`.
  - **T6.2 — liveness-409 guards on trigger-next, git-recover, delete-ACTIVE, clear.** These four endpoints could start a second orchestrator / run `git stash`+`checkout`+state-rewrite mid-phase / orphan a live orchestrator because they checked only a file-backed ACTIVE *row* (or nothing), not orchestrator liveness. Each now uses the standard `if lock_path and _check_orchestrator_liveness(lock_path): 409` idiom (inert when no `lock_path` is configured / the lock is not held, so existing tests are unaffected): trigger-next and git-recover require Stop-first; delete refuses an ACTIVE row while live; clear requires `{"force": true}` while live.
  - **T6.3 — write state before spawn in launch / switch-project.** Both spawned **then** wrote `pipeline_state.json`, so the child could read the previous project's state at startup and a post-spawn write failure returned `{ok:false}` while a pipeline was already running. Both now write state (atomic) **before** `_spawn_orchestrator`; a pre-spawn write failure aborts the spawn. This also closes the window the T6.1 lock-lift would otherwise widen (the child now reads disk state *after* the parent committed it).
  - **T6.4 — idempotent reviewer-PASS merge + durable `phase_merged` marker (Decision #2; highest-risk, landed last).** A crash after the phase-branch merge commit landed but before the phase advanced re-entered the reviewer-PASS block (status RUNNING + `current_agent=reviewer` → reviewer re-PASSes) and re-ran `git merge`, false-escalating `ERR_MERGE_FAILED` on already-complete work. The fix writes a durable `phase_merged` (+ `merge_base_branch`) marker into `phase_state` **only after a confirmed rc-0 merge**; on re-entry the marker skips the whole stage/commit/merge and the roadmap flip + suggestions append (which aren't idempotent). The marker self-clears because `phase_state.json` is deleted on advance and is not in `reset_phase`'s preserve-list. A `git merge-base --is-ancestor` backstop covers the case where the marker didn't persist. **Refinements (validation):** (B2) a re-merge of an already-merged branch returns rc 0 ("Already up to date"), *not* a failure — so the marker prevents the wasteful re-run/re-amend in the common case while the `--is-ancestor` guard prevents the *false* `ERR_MERGE_FAILED` only in the branch-recreated-empty sub-case (both are needed; they cover different sub-cases); (B3) the already-shipped T4.4 `_flip_roadmap_checkbox_or_escalate` was modified to **skip its `git commit --amend` when the checkbox is already `[x]`** — an amend with no content delta still rewrites the merge-commit SHA (and moves the `--force` phase-complete tag) on every restart; (B4) the marker persists the resolved base so the `--is-ancestor` check is correct under base-branch auto-detect drift; (B8) `merge_result` stays bound on every branch (synthesized `CompletedProcess(returncode=0)` on the skip paths) so the unchanged `if merge_result.returncode != 0:` line never `UnboundLocalError`s and the merge-failure-diagnosis AST test stays green. **No new pipeline state** — VALID_STATES stays 8.

  *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` — `_read_queue` + new `_quarantine_queue_file`, `_select_next_queue_project` (wrapper/`_inner` split + sanitization), `_promote_answered_escalations`, `_skip_and_requeue_group` / `_get_all_descendants` / `_move_group_atomically` (subscript tolerance), `acquire_lock` (idempotency), `update_symlink` (transactional), `main()` (lock-lift), the reviewer-PASS merge block (marker + is-ancestor + flip/suggestions gating), `_flip_roadmap_checkbox_or_escalate` (amend-skip). `ui/server.py` — `_spawn_orchestrator` (OSError guard, fd close, `confirm_lock`) + `_SPAWN_LOCK_CONFIRM_*` constants, liveness guards on `_queue_run_trigger_next_logic` / git-recover / `delete_queue_entry` / `post_queue_clear`, write-before-spawn + `confirm_lock=True` on launch / switch / resume / recover. *Tests (TDD — 40):* `test_defensive_p6_queue_parsing.py` (11), `test_defensive_p6_lock_symlink.py` (7), `test_defensive_p6_merge_idempotency.py` (10, hybrid source-structure + behavioral seams), `tests/test_defensive_p6_server_liveness.py` (12). *Tests removed:* none (additive surface; the merge-failure-diagnosis AST test was verified to stay green rather than replaced). *Docs:* CLAUDE.md (state-machine / queue / spawn / operational-constants sections), this entry, roadmap Phase-6 checkboxes. *Callouts (not actioned):* a UI label for the existing `queue_revive_project_missing` event is still deferred; an interactive `confirm_lock` timeout returns `{ok:false}` (matches Decision #1 "confirm before success" — the lock-lift makes the child take the lock in <1 s, so a 10 s timeout is a genuine failure signal); the residual microsecond CAS window and the deferred `queue_write_token` nonce are unchanged.

- **Phase 5 cron, path-resolution & runtime-state durability (T5.1–T5.5; session_cleanup reframed).** The defensive-hardening roadmap's Phase 5 — four cron/runtime modules that run under a bare system-cron environment and write the live runtime store, each failing silently (fail-safe), which is why the gaps went unnoticed. TDD red→green (33 new tests across four `test_phase5_*.py` files; the pipeline suite is **1150 passed, 0 regressions**, +33 over Phase 4's 1117).

  - **Reframe — `session_cleanup.py` had never pruned anything.** Verified live: it read a non-existent path (`OPENCLAW_ROOT/workspace-{agent}/sessions/sessions.json`) **and** a wrong schema (`store.get("sessions", [])`), while the real store is `OPENCLAW_ROOT/agents/{agent}/sessions/sessions.json` — a **flat dict keyed by sessionKey** (executor alone held 157 unpruned sessions). The prune loop is rewritten to the real path + schema. **Removed** the dead `workspace-{agent}` path, the `store.get("sessions", [])` access, and the bogus `store["count"]` write (the real schema has no such key) — no test covered that behaviour, so nothing to delete there.
  - **T5.3/T5.4 folded into the rewrite (Decision #9).** The pruned index is now written **atomically** (module-local `_atomic_write_json` — `mkstemp`+`os.replace`, matching `gate_scripts/utils.py`) **before** any transcript is deleted (persist-before-delete: an interrupted run orphans transcript files rather than corrupting the index). A session's `.jsonl` **and its `.trajectory.jsonl` / `.trajectory-path.json` siblings** are deleted, only ever inside the agent's own `sessions/` dir (`realpath`+`commonpath` boundary check; `sessionFile` basename collapses any out-of-dir path). A missing / `0` / non-numeric / **bool** / seconds-magnitude `updatedAt` is now **kept-and-warned** (`_is_valid_ms_timestamp`, ≥ `1e12`) instead of defaulting to `0` → epoch → deleted-regardless (the original unsafe default). The escalation agent stays exempt.
  - **Operator decision — delete live at 30d.** No opt-in / dry-run knob: the first 02:00 cron run after deploy bulk-prunes every >30-day session (~150+) and its transcript. Expected and accepted.
  - **T5.1 — cron env hardening (self-load + fail-loud guard).** New `env_resolvers.load_repo_env_file()` populates **unset** canonical vars from `<repo>/.env` (`setdefault` — a sourced/explicit env still wins; missing file → no-op), called at the top of both cron modules so a bare cron environment still resolves the roots. Each `main()` then **refuses to run (exit 1) when `OPENCLAW_ROOT` is not a directory** (heartbeat hands that root to any orchestrator it restarts, so a broken root must not pass). `session_cleanup`'s `RotatingFileHandler` gained `delay=True` so the guard is reachable (the log lives under `OPENCLAW_ROOT`; eager open would crash at import on a bad root). **Operator decision — script self-loads `.env`** (over an `install.sh` crontab rewrite), so `install.sh`'s cron-migration block is unchanged — this reconciles, not ignores, Decision #11.
  - **T5.2 — heartbeat splits `JSONDecodeError` out of its catch-all.** A corrupt `pipeline_state.json` read **while the orchestrator is dead** now prints `[CRITICAL]` and exits 1 (recovery BLOCKED, visible) instead of being swallowed by the broad `except Exception` as a vague `[ERROR]` and silently skipped every 30 min. The same read while the orchestrator is **alive** is a benign `[WARN]` + return (the live process owns and rewrites the file). `SystemExit` bypasses the broad `except`; the `finally` still releases the lock.
  - **T5.5 — queue-version type safety + CAS purity.** `read_queue_version` rejects `bool` (`isinstance(True, int)` is `True`, so `queue_version: true` previously read as `1` and `bump` wrote `True+1` with the type flipped). `mutate_queue` now **raises `RuntimeError`** if a `mutate_fn` mutates the `queue_version` key — the one provable closure-purity violation (the key is owned solely by `bump_queue_version`). All real CAS closures are pure, so the existing queue-CAS suites (15 tests) pass unchanged. Documenting the broader purity invariant at every call site stays Phase 7 (T7.3).

  *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/env_resolvers.py` (`load_repo_env_file`); `autodev/pipeline/heartbeat_cron.py` (`.env` self-load, `json.load` split, new `main()` guard); `autodev/pipeline/session_cleanup.py` (rewrite: `_atomic_write_json` / `_is_valid_ms_timestamp` / `_transcript_stem` / `_delete_session_transcripts` helpers + new `cleanup_sessions` body + `main()` guard + `delay=True`); `autodev/pipeline/queue_semantics.py` (`read_queue_version` bool guard, `mutate_queue` purity check). *Tests (TDD — 33):* `test_phase5_session_cleanup.py` (11), `test_phase5_cron_env_guard.py` (9), `test_phase5_heartbeat_corrupt_state.py` (3), `test_phase5_queue_version_bool.py` (10). *Tests removed:* none (`cleanup_sessions` had zero functional coverage — only `rotate_pipeline_logs` was tested, and that is untouched). *Docs:* CLAUDE.md (Session-TTL row, heartbeat-cron paragraph, F9 purity note) + this entry + roadmap Phase-5 checkboxes/decision reconciliation. *Callouts (not actioned):* prd-creator/roadmap-converter session GC is still uncovered (the `AGENTS` list stays the 4 pipeline agents to avoid surprise Ideas-session deletion); the ~5 inline `mkstemp+os.replace` copies across the pipeline could be DRY'd into a shared helper in a separate refactor; a crash between index-write and transcript-delete orphans transcript files that won't be re-GC'd (accepted — better than a corrupt index).

- **Phase 4 orchestrator fail-closed & parse safety (T4.1–T4.6, T4.8–T4.10; T4.7 de-scoped).** The defensive-hardening roadmap's Phase 4 — swallowed-error and unguarded-parse paths in `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` that, on failure, corrupted state during the operator's own recovery commands or mis-routed the pipeline. Nine fixes, all fail-closed (route to the existing escalation channel rather than proceed blind), TDD red→green (31 new tests across four `test_defensive_p4_*.py` files). The roadmap's per-task line anchors were all stale (+22 to +1432 lines) and were re-validated against source.

  - **T4.1 — reset paths fail closed on a git error (Decision #4).** `reset_phase` / `reset_execution` swallowed a `git` `CalledProcessError` and then unconditionally wiped outputs, zeroed counters, and transitioned to RUNNING — so the operator's RESET_PHASE/RESET_EXECUTION recovery command "succeeded" while leaving a **corrupt tree**, and charged the reset budget anyway. Both now **return `bool`** and, on a git failure, route to escalation (`ERR_RESET_PHASE_GIT_FAILED` / `ERR_RESET_EXECUTION_GIT_FAILED`) **before** any destructive step. The escalation-budget increment is gated on confirmed success: `reset_execution`'s is internal (skipped by the early return); the `RESET_PHASE` dispatch handler and `nuclear_reset_phase` now increment `escalation_resets` / `nuclear_resets` only when `reset_phase()` returns `True` (capturing the reason first, since a successful reset clears `last_error_code`). `nuclear_reset_phase` keeps its `nuclear_reset` observability event but emits it only on success, carrying the phase pointer captured before the reset.
  - **T4.2 — blame-analyst wrong-shape 200 escalates instead of mis-attributing.** A well-formed-but-wrong-shape 200 (an OpenAI-style `{"error": …}` body a loaded llama-server returns) yielded empty content → `ValueError` → the broad `except` → Layer-2 heuristics defaulting to `impl`, burning a real executor retry on an infra blip. `run_blame_attribution` now validates the response shape (`choices` present/non-empty/list; non-empty content) **before** destructuring and routes a missing/empty shape to the same `unknown`/escalate branch as malformed JSON. The escalation-advisory sibling already returned `None` on any wrong shape (its broad `except`), so it was **verified correct and left unchanged** (the roadmap's "fix opportunistically" note — no behaviour bug there).
  - **T4.3 — `current_phase.json` read on advance is guarded (both sites).** The post-PENDING read in `_advance_to_next_pending_phase` had no try/except (the outer `except` only caught `CalledProcessError`): a truncated/corrupt file raised an uncaught `JSONDecodeError`, and a valid-but-shapeless file silently advanced to `current_phase=0, raw_id=""` (branch `phase/`, colliding session keys). Both reads now route corrupt/shapeless content to the existing F4 `ERR_PHASE_RESOLVER_FAILED` escalation. **Validation correction:** the roadmap's claimed "mirror" was mis-identified by the scoping pass as the already-guarded metrics-row read; the real mirror is the **startup** read in `_run_startup_planner_phase_zero_and_branch` (corrupt was already caught by that method's broad `except`; the empty-shape gap is now closed there too).
  - **T4.4 — failed roadmap-checkbox flip escalates fail-closed (Decision #5), and a latent dead-flip bug is fixed.** **Significant finding:** the inline checkbox flip in the reviewer-PASS merge block used bare `re.match`, but `re` was never imported at module scope (only `import re as _re` exists in two unrelated methods) — so the flip **always raised `NameError`, swallowed** as "Failed to update roadmap". Since `phase_resolver.py` keys phase completion off the `[x]` checkbox (and `_mark_roadmap_phase` only flips on SKIP/PROCEED, never normal PASS), this is precisely the silent re-run / `ERR_MERGE_FAILED`-on-empty-branch symptom the roadmap describes. The fix adds the missing module-level `import re` (reviving the flip) **and** extracts `_flip_roadmap_checkbox_or_escalate()`, which on a non-git failure routes to escalation (`ERR_ROADMAP_CHECKBOX_FAILED`, "merge succeeded, roadmap checkbox couldn't be flipped — fix and resume") and returns `False` so the caller skips tag + advance; a git `CalledProcessError` re-raises to the existing outer handler. (No double-flip risk — the inline path was the only normal-PASS mechanism.)
  - **T4.5 — corrupt banked answer surfaces instead of silently STOPping.** A corrupt `pending_escalation_command.json` was consumed as `command="STOP"` and the file deleted — quiet loss of the operator's RESET_PHASE/PROCEED/SKIP intent. `_apply_pending_escalation_command` now emits the existing `escalation_command_invalid` event, **leaves the file in place** (so the operator can re-bank a valid answer the server atomically overwrites), and applies no command.
  - **T4.6 — `_detect_base_branch` is bounded and fails safe.** Its four `git` probes had no `timeout=` (a wedged git hangs the whole pipeline while the exclusive lock is held → heartbeat-cron can't restart it) and no guard for a missing git binary / dangling `cwd` (uncaught `FileNotFoundError`). Each probe now carries `timeout=_BASE_BRANCH_PROBE_TIMEOUT` (10 s) and any `FileNotFoundError`/`OSError`/`TimeoutExpired` falls back to `"main"`.
  - **T4.8 — reviewer token totals accumulate (shared helper).** The reviewer token-capture site **overwrote** `reviewer_tokens_acc` on every reviewer re-invocation (CONTRACT_FAILURE / `*_UNVERIFIED` / multi-pass) while the planner/executor sites accumulated — under-reporting reviewer cost. Extracted `_accumulate_role_tokens(role, jsonl_path)` (per-key sum, non-dict-accumulator guard) and routed all three duplicated blocks through it, fixing the reviewer path and removing the duplication that let it drift.
  - **T4.9 — empty-string llama `baseUrl` treated as absent.** `.get("baseUrl", default)` returns `""` when the key exists-but-empty, yielding a relative URL. Extracted the shared `_llama_chat_completions_url()` resolver (`… or f"{_LLAMA_ORIGIN}/v1"`) used by the blame-analyst and escalation-advisory paths, removing two duplicated blocks.
  - **T4.10 — deleted project dir on revive is surfaced.** When a queued project's directory was deleted, `makedirs` failed (swallowed) and the missing pending file read identically to "no banked command" — the answer was silently lost. `_apply_pending_escalation_command` now logs loudly and emits a new `queue_revive_project_missing` event when the project root is gone (or `.autodev/pipeline` can't be created), distinguishable from the normal no-bank case.
  - **T4.7 — de-scoped (operator-confirmed latent-only).** The validated `hooks_token` inconsistency only manifests if the gateway token is written in the top-level form; the live `openclaw.json` uses the nested `hooks.token` form, so the orchestrator's direct reads already resolve correctly. Not an active bug → not actioned (recorded, not silently omitted).

  *Changed:* `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` only — module-level `import re` + `_BASE_BRANCH_PROBE_TIMEOUT`; `_detect_base_branch` (T4.6); `_llama_chat_completions_url` helper + 2 call sites (T4.9); `run_blame_attribution` Layer-1 shape-check (T4.2); `_accumulate_role_tokens` helper + 3 call sites (T4.8); `_apply_pending_escalation_command` (T4.5 + T4.10); `_advance_to_next_pending_phase` + `_run_startup_planner_phase_zero_and_branch` reads (T4.3); `_flip_roadmap_checkbox_or_escalate` helper + merge-block call (T4.4); `reset_phase` / `reset_execution` (return bool, fail-closed) + `nuclear_reset_phase` + RESET_PHASE dispatch handler (T4.1). *Tests (TDD, red→green — 31):* `autodev/tests/test_defensive_p4_reset_failclosed.py` (T4.6 + T4.1), `test_defensive_p4_parse_safety.py` (T4.9 + T4.2 + T4.3), `test_defensive_p4_escalation_consume.py` (T4.5 + T4.10), `test_defensive_p4_advance_and_tokens.py` (T4.8 + T4.4). *Existing test updated to the new structure (not silenced):* `test_phase3_phase_outcome.py::test_nuclear_reset_records_outcome` — its fixed 2000-char source window overflowed after the T4.1 reorder/docstring growth; rewritten to scan the whole `nuclear_reset_phase` body (same invariant). Pipeline suite **1117 passed, 0 regressions**. *New error codes:* `ERR_RESET_PHASE_GIT_FAILED`, `ERR_RESET_EXECUTION_GIT_FAILED`, `ERR_ROADMAP_CHECKBOX_FAILED`. *New event:* `queue_revive_project_missing`. *Callouts (not actioned):* the two `import re as _re` locals are now redundant with the module import (left to stay targeted); leaving a corrupt banked file in place means the UI's existence-only `has_banked_answer` probe still shows "Answer banked" (a validity-aware probe is a server follow-up); a UI label for `queue_revive_project_missing` (`ui/index.html`) is deferred (event renders generically).

- **Browser preflight E2E tests: stale brand string after the Lullabeast rename.** `tests/test_browser_path_selector.py`'s first-run gate matched the literal `"Continue to AutoDev →"` button (and the `txt.includes('Continue to AutoDev')` shell check), but the Lullabeast branding refactor (`ee9a499`, 2026-06-04) renamed the first-run button to `"Continue to Lullabeast →"` in `ui/index.html` **without** updating this test — so on a live, not-yet-configured server (first-run screen, no `nav`), `_wait_app_shell` never found either condition and the 4 preflight tests spun their full 90 s `wait_for_function` and timed out. This stayed hidden because the whole file `pytest.skip`s unless a server is reachable at `127.0.0.1:18790`, so full-suite runs without the live UI service silently skipped it. *Fix:* both checks are now **brand-agnostic** — `_dismiss_first_run_if_present` matches `re.compile(r"Continue to .+ →")` and `_wait_app_shell` matches the stable prefix `'Continue to '` — so a future rebrand can't re-break them. The product code (`index.html`) was already correct; this is a test-only drift fix. *Changed:* `tests/test_browser_path_selector.py` (`_dismiss_first_run_if_present`, `_wait_app_shell`). *Verified:* full file **9 passed** against the live first-run server (previously 4 failed / 5 passed). *Note:* found while triaging the 4 failures flagged during the Phase 3 regression sweep — they are this drift, not a Phase 3 regression.

- **Phase 3 server read-path & Ideas hardening (T3.1–T3.9).** The defensive-hardening roadmap's Phase 3 — a cluster of independent silent-bad-output / 500-on-misconfig / non-atomic-write bugs in `ui/server.py`'s read and Ideas paths, several fixed by copying a correct pattern that already existed elsewhere in the file. Nine fixes, TDD red→green (16 red + 3 characterization, all in `tests/test_phase3_server_hardening.py`).

  - **T3.1 — numeric config-key coercion in `load_config`.** A string in `ui/config.json` (a typo `"5 min"`, or even a valid `"300"`) flowed straight through the dual-source merge to a consumer's `float(config[...])` → `ValueError` 500 — and for the Ideas flows that 500 fired *after* the webhook had already enqueued, leaving a stuck "Working on your request…" placeholder. `load_config` now coerces `poll_timeout`/`poll_interval`/`ideas_idle_threshold` (`float`) and `port` (`int`) after the merge, degrading each malformed value to its `DEFAULTS` value (`port` is the one key consumed without a call-site `float()`). Reuses the existing env-override try/except shape.
  - **T3.2 — `_read_json_file` broadened exception net.** It caught only `FileNotFoundError`/`JSONDecodeError`, so a path that exists but can't be read as JSON (a directory, undecodable bytes, bad permissions) propagated as a 500 from read-tolerant endpoints (`get_state`, `post_command`, autostart, delete) — all of which treat a `None` return as "absent, degrade gracefully". Now `except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError): return None` (`FileNotFoundError` is an `OSError` subclass, so it stays covered). No caller relies on a raised exception to distinguish "unreadable" from "absent".
  - **T3.3 — preflight tolerates a missing `git` binary.** The `git --version` probe and the later branch-list block ran bare; a missing git raises `FileNotFoundError` (not a non-zero return), so `_run_preflight_checks` crashed with a 500 on exactly the misconfiguration it exists to diagnose. The probe is now wrapped in `try/except (FileNotFoundError, OSError)` → appends the existing `{"check":"git","status":"fail",…}` dict, and a `git_ok` flag short-circuits the two later git-dependent blocks so they can't re-raise.
  - **T3.4 — `/convert` & `/fix-roadmap-format` reject an empty roadmap.** Success was gated only on the `.done` sentinel, so a model refusal/truncation (empty draft) was accepted as HTTP 200 and **overwrote a prior good roadmap** in `session.json`. Both endpoints now validate draft *content* after the poll and raise a `502` retry **before** persisting, so the prior roadmap is preserved (`/convert` requires both `roadmap_draft.md` and `verification_draft.md` non-empty). Mirrors `/clarity-check`, which already validated its output.
  - **T3.5 — atomic `escalation_output.json` write.** The live operator-command channel was written with a plain truncating `open('w')` + `json.dump` (`.done` after), so a crash mid-write could hand the orchestrator a half-written command (RETRY/RESET/SKIP/PROCEED/STOP). It now uses the exact mkstemp + `os.replace` idiom of the already-hardened sibling `_write_pending_escalation_files` (unique temp, temp cleaned on failure), drops any stale `.done` before committing the payload, and writes the fresh `.done` last — so the sentinel always trails a complete payload.
  - **T3.6 — atomic `pipeline-project` symlink swap.** Three sites (`_repoint_pipeline_project_symlink`, `_run_preflight_checks`, the repo-init mode A/B sync) did `os.remove` then `os.symlink`, leaving a window with no link and dropping it entirely if `os.symlink` raised — while the orchestrator about to spawn depends on it. A new pure-Python helper `_atomic_symlink_swap(target, link_path)` creates the link under a unique temp name and `os.replace`s it over the target (atomic rename; cleans the temp + leaves the old link intact on failure); all three sites call it. No subprocess.
