  *(a) `resolveIdeasRootFromWorkspace` returned null without `OPENCLAW_ROOT`.* The agent-event-stream backstop (`recordPipelineActivityFromAgentEvent`) has no `workspaceDir` because it runs at host-level, and the production OpenClaw gateway systemd unit sets `HOME=/home/pi` but does NOT set `OPENCLAW_ROOT`. The resolver therefore returned null, the backstop silently no-op'd, and `assistant` streaming events during long model generations never refreshed the stamp — `stall_threshold` fired at ~120 s while the agent was actively generating. The Pipeline equivalent already had a `HOME/.openclaw` fallback via `resolveOpenClawStateDirFromEnv`; Ideas didn't. **Fix:** `resolveIdeasRootFromWorkspace` now falls back to `env["HOME"]/.openclaw` then `os.homedir()/.openclaw` when `OPENCLAW_ROOT` is empty, mirroring the pipeline chain. The agent-end handler's warn message was rewritten to surface all three resolution sources so future operators don't have to reverse-engineer a silent no-op.

  *(b) `npm run build` does not deploy.* The gateway loads `~/.openclaw/extensions/autodev-pipeline-signals/dist/index.js`, but `npm run build` only updates the repo's own `dist/`. The two had drifted: the repo bundle was current (May 28) while the deployed extension was stale (May 20). My prior wrap-up restarted the gateway after only `npm run build`, which loaded the *stale* bundle — the regex fix was source-true but production-blind. **Fix:** new `npm run deploy` script (`autodev/plugin/package.json`) builds and copies `dist/index.js` directly into `${OPENCLAW_ROOT:-$HOME/.openclaw}/extensions/autodev-pipeline-signals/dist/index.js`, then prints the gateway-restart command. Falls back to a clear "run install.sh / openclaw plugins install" message on first-time installs. `install.sh` step 11 remains the canonical first-time installer; `npm run deploy` is the iterative-development primitive.

  *Plugin tests added.* `stall-detector.test.ts` gains one regression test (`agent event without OPENCLAW_ROOT falls back to HOME/.openclaw (Ideas)`) that mirrors the production systemd env shape — `{HOME: tmp}`, no `OPENCLAW_ROOT`. Tests now total 56 (was 55).

  *Pipeline equivalent.* `resolvePipelineArtifactsDirFromEnv` already had the same fallback chain via `resolveOpenClawStateDirFromEnv`, so this defect was Ideas-only. The asymmetry should not be re-introduced in future refactors.

- **Ideas chat false-timeout at 30 s (plugin session-key shape).** `autodev/plugin/src/utils.ts`'s `isIdeasSession`, `extractIdeasIdFromSessionKey`, and `parseIdeasTurnSession` only matched the bare `ideas:` prefix. The OpenClaw gateway delivers `hookCtx.sessionKey` with the `agent:{role}:` prefix (production form: `agent:prd-creator:ideas:{ideaId}:session-{n}`), so every Tier A hook for every real Ideas turn hit the Ideas branch's `sessionKey.startsWith("ideas:")` guard and silently skipped the stamp touch. Net effect: `prd_creator_activity.stamp` was never refreshed in production, the backend poller (which we just migrated to stamp-only Tier A liveness) saw zero activity, `startup_grace_seconds=30` expired, and a 408 fired while the agent was genuinely working. Caught live on the Untitled Balloon Popping Game chat session-13/14. Fixed by mirroring `isPipelineSession`'s dual-form check (bare + `agent:{role}:…`) in all three Ideas matchers; the inline `sessionKey.startsWith("ideas:")` at `stall-detector.ts:78` is replaced with `isIdeasSession(sessionKey)` for the same coverage. Plugin tests gained three production-form regression cases (`stall-detector.test.ts` — bare-form companions still pin the literal `ideas:` shape so future regressions in either direction are caught). Plugin rebuilt; `dist/index.js` ships alongside the source change. CLAUDE.md's existing "Plugin build & sessionKey shape" gotcha paragraph now lists the Ideas matchers alongside `isPipelineSession`.

### Added

- **Ideas chat 408 timeout recovery + reply auto-scroll + trailing-error cleanup.** Three connected fixes for the chat-UX gap visible in the "stacked red bubbles" bug report:

  *408 draft restoration (`ui/index.html`).* The submit-message `.catch` handler's three guards — `_gatewayFailed` flag on the assistant error bubble, `_gatewayFailed` flag on the user row, and `setInputText(t)` to restore the draft — previously fired only on 502/503 (gateway failures). They now also fire on 408 (real OpenClaw stalls, the path our recent `_poll_sentinel_with_idle_detect` migration anchors to true Tier A activity). After a timeout the user's draft text returns to the composer so a single press of Enter retries; the failed pair is dropped from `baseMsgs` on the next send so the chat stays clean instead of accumulating one red bubble per retry.

  *Server-side trailing-pair cleanup (`ui/server.py`).* New `_strip_trailing_failed_pairs(messages)` helper called inside `post_ideas_message` between the `pre_session` read and the pre-save write. Drops trailing `(user, assistant-with-error: true)` pairs (and orphan trailing error-only assistant bubbles) so a browser refresh after a 408/502/503 retry does not re-surface the stacked bubbles the client-side filter just hid. Non-trailing failed pairs are preserved as conversation history (deliberate policy — `test_strip_preserves_non_trailing_failed_pair` pins this).

  *Auto-scroll on reply arrival (`ui/index.html`).* New `useEffect` watching `messages` calls `messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth", block:"end"})` when the last message transitions to a non-pending assistant. The ref was already allocated (line ~4133) and attached to the chat list (line ~5626) but never scrolled. Trigger is *reply arrival only* — the guard `!last.pending` prevents bumping the user down while their own send sits queued, matching the user-stated intent ("when the actual output has been provided").

  *Tests added.* `tests/test_ui_ideas_timeout_recovery_contract.py` (5 static-regex contract tests pinning the three 408 branches + two scroll guarantees) and `tests/test_api_ideas_failed_turn_cleanup.py` (8 unit tests for the helper + 2 integration tests through `POST /api/ideas/{id}/message`). The existing `tests/test_ui_ideas_gateway_heal_contract.py` regexes were loosened so they assert 502/503 are *still in* the heal-eligible condition without over-pinning the now-expanded `(408 || 502 || 503)` shape; the 408 coverage is owned by the new file.

  *UI — still unchanged otherwise.* No new endpoints, no progressive "still working…" indicator, no copy changes to the existing pending bubble. Same policy as the upstream stamp-poll change: backend correctness first, surface stays as-is.

- **Ideas chat sentinel poll — stamp-only Tier A liveness.** `ui/server.py` `_poll_sentinel_with_idle_detect` now reads only `prd_creator_activity.stamp` mtime as the Tier A liveness source and returns the existing `PollResult` dataclass from `autodev/pipeline/sentinel_poller.py` with `succeeded` / `stalled` / `no_first_activity` / `timeout` reasons. Mirrors the pipeline's `poll_for_sentinel` two-knob design: `ideas_startup_grace` (pre-first-activity tolerance) and `ideas_idle_threshold` (post-first-activity stall threshold) are now distinct, semantically aligned with `AUTODEV_STARTUP_GRACE_*` and `AUTODEV_STALL_TIMEOUT_*`. The poller respects `attempt_start_wall` so a stamp left behind by a prior turn cannot fire `"stalled"` on the first iteration of the next turn.

  *Removal:* `_resolve_prd_creator_jsonl` and `_idea_workspace_activity_mtime` deleted wholesale — they were the JSONL- and artifact-mtime fallbacks the previous poller used, both strictly weaker than the stamp. No legacy stubs. `tests/test_ideas_stamp_detect.py` is rewritten so its name finally matches what it tests; the JSONL-liveness `TestPollSentinelStampDetect` class is replaced with six stamp-liveness tests covering each `PollResult` reason plus the boundary between the two knobs, and a new `test_jsonl_and_artifact_helpers_are_deleted` regression guard.

  *Plugin: `autodev/plugin/src/stall-detector.ts` `recordPipelineActivityFromAgentEvent` gains an Ideas branch* — the agent-event-stream subscription now refreshes the Ideas stamp for `ideas:`-prefixed sessions in addition to the existing pipeline coverage. Without this backstop, Ideas would go dark whenever OpenClaw failed to fire one of the typed `model_call_*` / `after_tool_call` hooks while pipeline sessions stayed covered. `autodev/plugin/src/utils.ts` `resolveIdeasRootFromWorkspace` takes an optional `env` arg so tests can pass a custom env without monkey-patching `process.env`. Plugin rebuilt; `dist/index.js` ships alongside the source change.

  *Call-site mock fix:* `tests/test_api_ideas_annotations.py::test_late_done_reconciliation_after_poll_false` and the two `_poll_sentinel_with_idle_detect` stubs in `tests/test_api_ideas_prd_section_diff.py` were returning truthy tuples (`(False, "poll_timeout")` and `True, ""`), so `if not sentinel_found:` never took the failure branch — the tests passed by accident. They now return `PollResult` instances; the late-recovery test additionally asserts via spy that the `_late_done_valid_for_attempt` branch was actually consulted.

  *UI:* unchanged. The existing "Working on your request…" indicator stays as-is — once the backend timeout is anchored to true Tier A activity, false timeouts disappear and the existing spinner becomes trustworthy on its own. No new endpoints, no progressive feedback text, no JS changes.

- **P1 Stage D — N→N-1 regression check + contract-shape counter consolidation + Hygiene H1.** Close the P0 loop where per-phase behavioural verification never looks backward: phase 5 silently breaking phase 2's user-observable behaviour was invisible to the framework. The reviewer now executes the immediately-preceding completed phase's `how_to_check` recipe alongside the current phase's, and emits a structured `regression_verification` block; the gate validates both content and shape; rejections route to the executor with `criterion_source: "regression_prior_phase"` so self-heal feedback names the regressed feature explicitly. Stage D iterates exactly one phase back — full prior-phase iteration with recipe-output caching is deferred to P3 Stage B.

  *Resolver:* `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/phase_resolver.py:parse_roadmap` tracks the most recent `[x]`-status phase as it walks (escalated `[!]` and skipped `[-]` phases never update the tracker — they did not land). `current_phase.json` gains two new keys: `prior_phase_raw_id` (most recent completed predecessor's raw id, or `null` when there is none) and `prior_phase_how_to_check` (the predecessor's `how_to_check` text, or `null` when the predecessor lacks a behavioural block). Inline comment pins the N→N-1 design decision on-site to prevent accidental promotion to multi-step iteration.

  *Reviewer gate:* `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/reviewer_gate.py` gains `_check_regression_verification(data, current_phase)` (shape validator — mirror of `_check_behavioral_verification`, deliberately reuses `_REQUIRED_BEHAVIORAL_EVIDENCE_KEYS` and `_MIN_BEHAVIORAL_EVIDENCE_ANCHORS` so both anchor types share the same anchor-quality bar) and `_synthesize_regression_blocking_issue(data, current_phase)` (synthesises ONE entry per regression failure — regression is one logical failure, not one-per-evidence). The synthesiser's idempotency keys on `criterion_source == "regression_prior_phase"` so it coexists with the behavioural synthesiser when both flavours fail with an empty initial `blocking_issues`. New verdict `REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED` (shape failure → re-invoke reviewer); new error code `ERR_REGRESSION_PRIOR_PHASE` (content failure when regression is the sole failing dimension); rejection branch wires `regression_rejection` alongside `behavioral_rejection` so the augmented payload write-back fires for either flavour.

  *Orchestrator handler consolidation:* `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` collapses the prior per-flavour `VISUAL_UNVERIFIED` and `BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED` handlers into a single parameterised `elif gate_result in ("VISUAL_UNVERIFIED", "BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED", "REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED"):` branch driving a pooled `reviewer_unverified_retries` counter (cap 2 across all three). Per-verdict instruction copy is resolved via a local `_UNVERIFIED_INSTRUCTIONS` dict; the phase_state writeback uses the new pooled `unverified_instruction` field; log lines name the firing verdict so operators distinguish visual / behavioural / regression in stderr and the activity feed. The verdict→agent diagnostic dispatch table adds `"REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED": "reviewer"`. **Removal:** `reviewer_visual_retries`, `reviewer_behavioral_retries`, `visual_instruction`, `behavioral_instruction` are deleted wholesale — pooling is the anti-sprawl design now that a third contract-shape verdict exists. The `criterion_source` enum docstring in `_write_reviewer_failure_context` is extended with `regression_prior_phase`; runtime path is unchanged because `_enrich_blocking_issue_with_criterion_default` only fills missing values.

  *Hygiene H1 (folded in):* `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/utils.py` exports two shared predicates — `phase_has_behavioral_block(current_phase)` (replaces the duplicate `_requires_behavioral_verification` in `reviewer_gate.py` and `_phase_has_behavioral_block` in `executor_gate.py`) and `requires_regression_verification(current_phase)` (the Stage D predicate that drives the new branch). Both gates import from utils and the duplicates are deleted. The `TestRequiresBehavioralVerification` class in `test_reviewer_gate_behavioral_verification.py` pinned the gate-local symbol and is removed; `test_gate_helpers.TestPhaseHasBehavioralBlock` pins the contract at its new canonical location.

  *Agent docs.* `autodev/agents/reviewer/AGENTS.md` Inputs section lists `prior_phase_raw_id` / `prior_phase_how_to_check`; the Output Contract JSON example gains a `regression_verification` block; the `criterion_source` enum gains `"regression_prior_phase"`; a new field doc describes the regression block in full (including the locked semantic that `prior_phase_how_to_check_followed: False` is treated identically to `cannot_verify`). `autodev/agents/executor/AGENTS.md` Inputs section flags the new `prior_phase_how_to_check` field; the line-18 blocking-issues guidance and the Scenario B block explain that a `criterion_source: "regression_prior_phase"` rejection requires re-executing BOTH the current and prior recipes after the targeted fix.

  *Tests added.* `autodev/tests/test_gate_helpers.py` (10 tests for the two shared predicates), `autodev/tests/test_phase_resolver_prior_phase.py` (6 tests for the resolver's new tracking), `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_gate_regression_prior_phase.py` (12 tests covering shape validation, content rejection, dual-synthesizer coexistence, idempotency, the `followed: False == cannot_verify` semantic, and `prior_phase_raw_id` mismatch detection), `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_regression_unverified.py` (5 source-level tests for the parameterised handler + removal-completeness for the legacy counter names). `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_behavioral_unverified.py` rewritten to assert on the pooled counter; filename preserved so operators searching for "behavioral" still find the surface. `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_routing_dispatch.py::KNOWN_VERDICTS` extended with `"REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED"` so the existing dispatch-handler invariant covers the new verdict too.

  *Docs.* `CLAUDE.md` operational-constants table collapses the two prior contract-shape rows into one pooled row. `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` reviewer-gate section updates the validation-checks pseudocode (adds REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED shape check and the regression-rejection branch); the verdict→counter mapping collapses to the pooled entry; the `criterion_source` enum is extended; two new error-code paragraphs (`ERR_REGRESSION_UNVERIFIED`, `ERR_REGRESSION_PRIOR_PHASE`) document the new dimension with a forward reference to P3 Stage B for full iteration.

- **P1 Stage C — PRD-verbatim grep gate.** Close the P0 loop where the planner emits `pass_criteria[].traces_to: "prd_verbatim:<exact string>"` anchors but no automated check ever confirmed the literal string appeared in the build. The executor gate now walks git-tracked source (`git ls-files`) and runs `grep -F -l --` for each anchor; any anchor whose literal is absent from every tracked file fails the executor with `ERR_PRD_VERBATIM_MISSING`. Files are passed to grep in chunks of 500 paths to stay under ARG_MAX. The check is a no-op when the planner declares no `prd_verbatim:` anchors and when `planner_output.json` is absent — it rides inside the existing `if planner_data is not None:` guard, sharing the defensive shape of the tdd cross-check that precedes it. On failure the gate writes `executor_gate_detail.json` with `{"gate_error": "ERR_PRD_VERBATIM_MISSING", "missing_anchors": [...]}` so the orchestrator can merge the missing anchors into `failure_context.json` for the next executor retry; attribution is `impl`.

  *New upstream contract.* PRDs that specify literal user-facing strings (taglines, button labels, error messages, CLI flag names, endpoint paths, exact API response strings) must list them under a `### Verbatim Strings` subsection nested inside `## Functional Requirements` of `prd.md`. Critically, this is a `###` subsection — NOT a 13th top-level `## Verbatim Strings` section, which would break the 12-section heading parser in `autodev/agents/prd-creator/AGENTS.md`. The planner reads the PRD directly and emits `prd_verbatim:` anchors for the strings that must appear character-for-character in code; the executor gate enforces them.

  *Files touched.* `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/executor_gate.py` gains three module-level helpers (`_extract_prd_verbatim_anchors`, `_tracked_files`, `_check_prd_verbatim_anchors`) and one wiring call inside `evaluate_executor`'s existing planner-data block. `autodev/agents/prd-creator/AGENTS.md` adds the `### Verbatim Strings` subsection contract with explicit warning against a 13th top-level section. `autodev/agents/planner/AGENTS.md` line 51 adds the enforcement note under the `prd_verbatim:` anchor bullet. `autodev/skill-library/roadmap-converter/roadmap-generation/SKILL.md` adds a "Preserving PRD Verbatim Strings" section directing the converter not to paraphrase literals away when summarising functional requirements into phase descriptions. `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` documents `ERR_PRD_VERBATIM_MISSING` in `§7 Gate Scripts → Executor Output Gate` adjacent to `ERR_UNACCOUNTED_DELETION`.

  *Tests added.* `autodev/tests/test_executor_gate_prd_verbatim.py` — nine tests covering the happy path (anchor present), the failure path (anchor missing, with `executor_gate_detail.json` payload assertion), the tracked-only contract (anchor in `node_modules/` does not count), the no-op path (no `prd_verbatim:` anchors → skip), both directions of `grep -F` literal semantics (regex metachars match themselves verbatim; regex-only matches do not count), multi-anchor success, multi-anchor partial failure (only missing anchors reported), and the defensive guard for absent `planner_output.json`. Real `git init` + `git add` + `git commit` per test rather than subprocess mocking, so the helpers under test (`subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files"])`, `subprocess.run(["grep", "-F", ...])`) operate on real on-disk state.

- **P1 Stage B — Cutover-hygiene test fixture migration + recents self-cleaning.** The `test_recents_appear_after_preflight` browser test silently `pytest.skip`'d under P0 Stage C's strict-mode preflight because its fixture was missing the Behavioral Verification block in `roadmap.md` and the entire `verification.md` file. Silent skips in CI mask exactly the regressions they exist to catch. Stage B brings the fixture to the canonical P0-compliant shape, narrows the surviving skip path to the one sanctioned case (CI without a full OpenClaw workspace install), and converts every other skip into a `pytest.fail` so a future regression surfaces loud. While here, the scope was extended to fix a separate ongoing pain: the recents JSON (`~/.openclaw/ui_recent_projects.json`) accumulates stale `/tmp/...` entries from every test run that exercises preflight, and the HTML `<datalist>` exposes no per-row remove affordance, so the user had no in-app way to clear the mess.

  *New API surface:*
  - `DELETE /api/setup/recent-projects` — body `{"path": "<absolute path>"}` removes a single entry. Idempotent (removing a path not in the list returns `removed: false` with status 200, not an error). Path is normalised via `os.path.realpath(os.path.expanduser(...))` so callers may submit `~/foo` / `/a/./b` / etc. and still target the canonical stored entry.
  - `POST /api/setup/recent-projects/prune` — no body. Sweeps every entry whose `path` is not an existing directory on disk (`os.path.isdir`). Returns `{removed_count, removed_paths, projects}`. Malformed entries (non-dict, missing/empty `path`) are also treated as dead and removed. Designed to be called from any test that mutates recents: the test removes its tmpdir, then calls prune, and the recents JSON self-cleans.

  Both handlers reuse the existing `_read_recent_projects` and `_write_recent_projects_atomic` helpers — no new file-format or locking surface.

  *Browser test changes (`tests/test_browser_path_selector.py::test_recents_appear_after_preflight`):*
  - Roadmap fixture extended with the four-line Behavioral Verification block. The `> Test:` line now uses the colon form (`_validate_roadmap_content`'s regex rejects the period form — proven empirically).
  - Adjacent `verification.md` written with all five required sections (mirrors the canonical shape used by the sibling `test_queue_add_autorepairs_git`).
  - Old "any failing check → skip" replaced with: `pytest.fail` on any non-`workspace-{agent}` failure, `pytest.skip` only when *every* failure is workspace-related (i.e., the CI environment legitimately lacks OpenClaw workspaces). The `urllib.error.HTTPError` branch flips from skip to `pytest.fail` for the same reason — an HTTP error is a real server bug.
  - `finally` block removes the test's tmpdir and POSTs `/api/setup/recent-projects/prune` so the recents entry created by preflight does not leak to the next run.

  *Tests added:* `tests/test_api_setup_recent_projects.py` — 5 tests for the DELETE endpoint (single-entry removal, idempotency on missing path, realpath normalisation, 422 on empty body, order preservation) and 5 tests for the prune endpoint (removes dead entries, keeps live entries, no-op on empty list, idempotency on second call, malformed entries swept).

  *Docs:* `plans/Active/post-p0-ordered-roadmap.md` updated to mark P1 Stage B as delivered. `CLAUDE.md` is unchanged — these endpoints are UI-side utilities that don't impact pipeline state or the orchestration model documented there.

- **P1 Stage A — Always inject `integration-wiring` + `testing-quality` base skills.** **⚠️ Superseded within `[Unreleased]` by the "P1 Stage A refactor" entry under `### Changed` at the top of this section — the outcome (universal rules on every phase) is retained, but the delivery mechanism described below (always-injected base skills) was replaced by AGENTS.md placement before anything shipped. Kept for history; read the Changed entry for the as-shipped behavior.** `SkillManager.inject_skill` now writes two base skills (`integration-wiring/{role}/SKILL.md` and `testing-quality/{role}/SKILL.md`) into the agent workspace on every phase regardless of the roadmap prefix, alongside the existing phase-prefix discipline skill. The base skills encode universal rules (the "read the entrypoint before wiring" rule and TDD discipline) that previously fired only when a phase carried the `INTEGRATION` / `TEST` prefix — i.e., almost never on real projects. P0's behavioural-verification gate caught the failures these rules would have prevented up front; Stage A removes that ceiling.

  *Implementation:*
  - `autodev/pipeline/skill_manager.py` exposes `SkillManager.BASE_DISCIPLINES = ("integration-wiring", "testing-quality")`. `inject_skill` is restructured to clean the workspace exactly once at method start, then write the base skills in a loop followed by the conditional phase-prefix skill. Each skill write emits a `[SKILL] Status=loaded base=true|false` log line so operators can audit which layer landed. A missing base-skill source does not strip the rest of the call (graceful per-skill degradation); kill switches (`pipeline.skills.enabled`, `{role}_skills_enabled`) still suppress both base and prefix.
  - `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` `_record_injected_skill` filters `BASE_DISCIPLINES` out of the workspace listing before deriving the recorded discipline name. The `phase_state.skill_injected` field continues to surface only the *variable* phase-prefix discipline (metrics row, snapshot endpoint, UI label all preserved unchanged) — base skills are constant and would be reporting noise.

  *Workspace contract change:* Each `workspace-{role}/skills/` directory now holds 2–3 subdirectories per phase (2 base + optional 1 prefix) instead of 0–1. OpenClaw's `loadWorkspaceSkillEntries` already walks the entire tree, so the multi-subdirectory layout requires no OpenClaw config change.

  *Tests added:* `autodev/tests/test_skill_manager_base_skills.py` (9 tests covering mapped/unmapped/empty-prefix paths, per-role isolation, wipe-between-phases preservation, kill-switch behaviour, missing-base-source graceful degradation, and the one-log-line-per-skill format) and `autodev/tests/test_record_injected_skill_filters_base.py` (3 tests pinning the orchestrator filter). `autodev/tests/test_skill_manager.py` updated: 5 existing tests now assert the multi-skill workspace shape, and the shared `build_manager` helper seeds the base disciplines by default.

  *Docs:* `CLAUDE.md` "Skill Injection — End-to-End" section rewritten to describe the base-skills + phase-prefix model and the per-layer skip semantics. `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` §Skills Overview, Skill Resolution Algorithm, and `skill_injected` schema entry updated to match.

- **P0 Stage G — Self-heal feedback loop for behavioural rejections.** The reviewer-rejection retry path (primary) and escalation advisory (fallback) now consume the structured behavioural-verification context Stage F materialised. Without Stage G, the executor saw an empty `blocking_issues` list whenever the reviewer rejected on a behavioural verdict alone, forcing blind re-implementation; the escalation advisory could not quote the project's own pre-authored `failure_language`. Stage G closes both gaps. Strict, no `legacy_mode` opt-out.

  *Gate-side synthesis:*
  - `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/reviewer_gate.py` adds `_synthesize_behavioral_blocking_issues(data)` — when the reviewer records `behavioral_verification.verdict ∈ {fail, cannot_verify}` with an empty `blocking_issues` list, the gate synthesises one entry per evidence claim (`description = claim`, `attribution = "impl"`, `affected_file = file_or_screenshot_or_log`, `criterion_source = "behavioral"`, `criterion_id = "behavioral_evidence[<N>]"`). Idempotent — agent-populated lists are respected (defensive symmetry with reviewer AGENTS.md). `evaluate_reviewer` calls the helper on `behavioral_rejection` and persists the augmented payload back to `reviewer_output.json` atomically (`mkstemp` + `os.replace`) so the orchestrator's downstream read sees the canonical list and the on-disk file matches what `failure_context.json` carries. `apply_reviewer_routing` stays pure routing.

  *Orchestrator failure context plumbing:*
  - `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` `write_failure_context` adds two new top-level fields: `behavioral_verification_evidence` (the reviewer's recorded behavioural block when the reviewer is the failing agent; `None` otherwise so a stale prior verdict cannot pollute the executor's context) and `current_phase_behavioral_verification` (the phase contract's claimed block, always captured). New private helper `_load_current_phase_block()` performs the fresh read of `current_phase.json`. `_write_reviewer_failure_context` now routes each blocking issue through `_enrich_blocking_issue_with_criterion_default()` which applies the explicit `"free"` label to any entry arriving without `criterion_source` — gate-synthesised behavioural entries pass through unchanged with their pre-tagged `criterion_source` + `criterion_id`.

  *Escalation advisory:*
  - `_generate_escalation_advisory` system prompt adds a conditional clause: quote the project's pre-authored `failure_language` verbatim **only when** the user-message payload includes a non-null `behavioral_verification` block AND `reviewer_retries >= 2`. The data gating is load-bearing — the user-message `behavioral_verification` key is `None` when below threshold (executor self-heal has not been attempted yet), so the LLM cannot quote what isn't there. When the threshold is met, the payload carries `{failure_language, verdict, evidence_count}` derived from `failure_context.current_phase_behavioral_verification` + `failure_context.behavioral_verification_evidence` — a single materialised snapshot the advisory and the executor's self-heal pass share.

  *Reviewer AGENTS.md (defensive symmetry):*
  - `autodev/agents/reviewer/AGENTS.md` `blocking_issues` JSON schema example adds `criterion_source` (four-valued enum: `"behavioral" | "test" | "prd_verbatim" | "free"`) and `criterion_id` (anchor pointer; absent on `"free"` source). Two new validation-rule bullets document the fields. Same "raise the floor" pattern as executor `behavioral_smoke_artifacts` — AGENTS.md says do it; gate enforces it; gate also synthesises as the fallback for the empty-list case.

  *Stage E executor guidance is the trigger.* The executor AGENTS.md guidance (Stage E, lines 18 + 145) keys on `failure_context.blocking_issues[i].criterion_source == "behavioral"` to re-run the `how_to_check` procedure on retry. Stage G provides the data path; the executor's existing contract triggers off it.

  *Docs:* `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` §6.4 (failure_context.json schema) updated with the three new fields (two top-level + two per-blocking-issue) and an explanatory paragraph naming the self-heal feedback loop. `autodev/docs/ASSUMPTIONS.md` adds §J capturing the gate-side-vs-orchestrator-side write-back decision, the `criterion_id` format rationale (distinct from planner `traces_to`), the `failure_language` sourcing rule (materialised snapshot, not fresh read), the data-level `reviewer_retries >= 2` gate, and the explicit-`"free"` defaulting rationale.

  *Tests added (new files):* `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_gate_synthesize_blocking_issues.py` (9), `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_failure_context_behavioral.py` (8), `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_escalation_advisory_behavioral.py` (4), `autodev/tests/test_p0_stage_g_data_path_integration.py` (5). `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_routing_dispatch.py` extended with one regression-guard test pinning that the orchestrator's ROUTE_EXECUTOR branch does NOT call the gate's synthesis helper (synthesis lives in the gate; the orchestrator stays a consumer).

- **P0 Stages E + F — Agent guidance + gate enforcement of the Behavioral Verification contract.** Continues the Stage C+D rollout: agents now consume the new per-phase block and the gate enforces the structured evidence reviewer output. Strict — no legacy opt-out (`phase_intent_validated` boolean removed in the same change, see §I of `ASSUMPTIONS.md`).

  *Stage E — agent guidance updates:*
  - `autodev/agents/planner/AGENTS.md` adds `prd.md` and `verification.md` to the required reads (PRD-anchored pass criteria need the source-of-truth file), and introduces `pass_criteria[].traces_to` with the four canonical anchor forms (`tdd:<test_path>`, `behavior:user_observable`, `behavior:how_to_check`, `prd_verbatim:<exact substring>`). Free-floating paraphrases of the PRD are no longer acceptable.
  - `autodev/agents/executor/AGENTS.md` adds `prd.md` + `verification.md` reads, a new `behavioral_smoke_artifacts` field in the output contract (mirrors the existing `visual_smoke_artifacts` shape), a TDD-workflow step "execute the phase's `how_to_check` procedure as a final step and capture output under `.autodev/pipeline/behavioral-smoke/`", and a Scenario B extension instructing re-running `how_to_check` and re-capturing artifacts when a reviewer-rejection blocking issue carries `criterion_source: "behavioral"`.
  - `autodev/agents/reviewer/AGENTS.md` reorders inputs to PRD-first (`prd.md → verification.md → current_phase → planner_output → executor_output → phase_state`), removes `phase_intent_validated` from the output schema, and adds a structured `behavioral_verification: {verdict, evidence, how_to_check_followed}` object with the three-evidence-anchor minimum on `verdict: "pass"`. New review principle prepended to "What to Actually Review": **PRD is truth; the planner's `pass_criteria` is derivative.**

  *Stage F — gate enforcement:*
  - `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/reviewer_gate.py` adds `_requires_behavioral_verification(current_phase)` (content-driven sibling of `_is_visual_phase` — keys on a populated block in `current_phase.json`, not on raw-id prefix) and `_check_behavioral_verification(data)` (mirror of `_check_visual_verification`: returns a problem-string list, enforces verdict enum, three-anchor minimum on pass, workspace-bound path safety, on-disk existence). `evaluate_reviewer` now emits a new `BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED` verdict on contract-shape failure (non-`reviewer_retries`-consuming, mirror of `VISUAL_UNVERIFIED`). The legacy `not data.get("phase_intent_validated")` trigger in the validation block was **removed** and replaced by `behavioral_rejection` keying on `behavioral_verification.verdict ∈ {fail, cannot_verify}` — the structured signal anchored to evidence rather than the unverifiable self-attested boolean.
  - `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/executor_gate.py` adds `_load_current_phase` + `_phase_has_behavioral_block` helpers and a new validation phase that runs after the TDD-coverage cross-reference: when the phase carries a populated behavioural block, `executor_output.behavioral_smoke_artifacts` must be a non-empty list of `{path, description}` dicts whose paths satisfy the same `os.path.commonpath` workspace-bound check as the existing file_manifest validation and exist on disk. New error code: `ERR_BEHAVIORAL_ARTIFACTS_MISSING` (path traversal still uses `ERR_PATH_TRAVERSAL` for consistency with the file_manifest path).
  - `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py` extends the verdict→next-agent diagnostic table with `"BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED": "reviewer"` (so the `[REVIEWER_GATE]` log line names the right agent) and adds an explicit `BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED` handler mirroring the existing `VISUAL_UNVERIFIED` block: increments a separate `reviewer_behavioral_retries` counter (cap 2 before escalation), writes a `behavioral_instruction` field to `phase_state.json`, re-invokes the reviewer. The main `reviewer_retries` budget is preserved — a legitimate code-quality rejection on the next pass still drives the 3-pass attribution flow.
  - `autodev/plugin/src/before-finalize-handler.ts` `checkReviewer` drops the legacy `phase_intent_validated (boolean)` requirement and enforces the new structural shape: `behavioral_verification` must be an object with `verdict` (string), `evidence` (array), and `how_to_check_followed` (boolean). The plugin remains a pure-JSON soft pre-gate; semantic rules (≥3 anchors on `pass`, on-disk path existence, workspace bounds) stay in the hard `reviewer_gate.py`. Bundle regenerated via `npm run build` (`dist/index.js`).

  *Docs:* `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` §5 (Reviewer Agent) — schema example updated (`phase_intent_validated` → structured `behavioral_verification` object, plus `visual_verification` documented inline); validation algorithm updated with the new precedence (BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED runs after VISUAL_UNVERIFIED, before the validation block); `reviewer_behavioral_retries` documented alongside `reviewer_visual_retries` as the second non-`reviewer_retries`-consuming counter. `autodev/docs/ASSUMPTIONS.md` §I captures the two coupled Stage F decisions (content-driven check; hard removal of `phase_intent_validated`) and the plugin-shape-vs-gate-semantics split.

  *Tests added (new files):* `tests/test_p0_planner_agent_md.py` (4), `tests/test_p0_executor_agent_md.py` (5), `tests/test_p0_reviewer_agent_md.py` (5), `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_gate_behavioral_verification.py` (17), `autodev/tests/test_executor_gate_behavioral_artifacts.py` (7), `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_behavioral_unverified.py` (4). `autodev/tests/test_reviewer_routing_dispatch.py` extends `KNOWN_VERDICTS` with `BEHAVIORAL_UNVERIFIED` and adds the matching emitted-by-gate + referenced-in-orchestrator pair (parametrised audit then catches the new verdict automatically). `autodev/plugin/tests/before-finalize.test.ts` rewrites the reviewer cases for the new contract and adds three negative cases for the malformed-`behavioral_verification` shape. Fixture updates: `autodev/tests/conftest.py` `valid_reviewer_output` now ships the structured `behavioral_verification` object (the legacy `phase_intent_validated: true` line was dead data); a new `current_phase_with_behavioral` fixture writes the canonical Stage D shape for Stage F gate tests; `test_reviewer_gate_visual_verification.py` and `test_reviewer_infra_failure.py` strip the dead field from their inline fixtures. Final: `pytest autodev/tests/ -q` 635 passed; `pytest tests/ -q` 1697 passed / 14 skipped / 1 failure (`test_browser_path_selector.py::test_queue_add_autorepairs_git` — pre-existing Stage C consequence: the test creates a project without materialising `verification.md`, and Stage C's strict preflight now rejects that shape; unrelated to Stage E/F). `npm test` in `autodev/plugin/` 50 passed.

- **P0 Stages C + D — Behavioral Verification enforcement at the staging boundary, plus structured per-phase context for agents (strict, no legacy opt-out per P0 §2.9).**

  *Stage C — preflight refuses to stage a project without the new contract:*
  - `ui/server.py` extends `_validate_roadmap_content` to require a `**Behavioral Verification:**` block within 30 lines of every phase header, with three sub-bullets (`User-observable`, `How we'll check`, `If this fails, the user sees`) in any order, each with non-empty body. No `legacy=True` opt-out exists; the validator is strict from day one.
  - New `_validate_verification_content` enforces the project-level `verification.md` schema: required headings in canonical order (`# Verification`, `## Project type`, `## Entry point`, `## Public surface`, `## Verification stack`); each section's body non-empty; the `## Project type` token must be one of the canonical 9 (`web-app | http-api | cli | library | data-pipeline | game | automation | desktop-app | mobile-app`) with a shape check (single line, ≤40 chars, no markdown formatting characters); doc length capped at 80 lines. Unknown project types hard-fail per operator decision — extending the list later means editing `_VERIFICATION_CANONICAL_TYPES`.
  - `_preflight_materialize(repo, roadmap, prd, verification_content=None)` validates, conflict-checks against any on-disk `verification.md`, and atomically writes via the existing `_atomic_write_file` helper.
  - `_run_preflight_checks` gains a new `verification doc` row positioned after the roadmap-file check — fail when missing or invalid; the hint points the operator at the Ideas-screen regenerate flow.
  - New endpoint `POST /api/setup/validate-verification` mirrors `POST /api/setup/validate-roadmap` (body `{content: str}` → `{valid, errors[]}`).
  - `/api/setup/preflight`, `/api/setup/launch` (via `_run_init_project`), and `/api/setup/switch-project` all accept a `verification_content` body field. `_run_init_project` refuses to initialize a project when neither the body nor an on-disk `verification.md` is available (closes the strict-mode hole the original plan named only for `preflight`; recorded in `autodev/docs/ASSUMPTIONS.md` §G).
  - `ui/index.html` adds a `launchVerificationContent` state alongside `launchPrdContent`, updates `navigateToPreflightWithSeed(roadmap, prd, verification)` and the `continueSetup` caller, and updates `onRunPreflight` + `onLaunch` body construction. The preflight check list is already iterator-based, so the new rows render automatically.

  *Stage D — pipeline plumbing:*
  - `autodev/pipeline/gate_scripts/phase_resolver.py` `parse_roadmap` now performs a bounded scan from each phase header to the next (rather than stopping at the first non-`>` line) and extracts: `behavioral_verification: {user_observable, how_to_check, failure_language} | None`, `entry_criteria` (str), `exit_criteria_block` (str — distinct from the legacy `exit_criteria` list, which is preserved unchanged for reviewer-gate back-compat), `tdd_requirements` (list of `{file, description}` dicts), and `done_criteria` (list of checkbox strings). `validate_and_identify` writes a new `verification_path` field on `current_phase.json` pointing at `<project_root>/verification.md`.
  - `ui/roadmap_parser.parse_roadmap` adds the symmetric `behavioral_verification` extraction (or `None` for absent/partial blocks). A round-trip drift detector test (`tests/test_roadmap_parser.py::TestParserDriftDetector`) fails the build if the two parsers ever disagree on the canonical fixture; passes today, so the shared-library extraction allowed by P0 §4 is deferred.
  - `autodev/pipeline/webhook_client.py` default messages for planner / executor / reviewer now also reference `pipeline-project/prd.md` and `pipeline-project/verification.md` — PRD as source-of-truth for user intent, verification.md as the per-project exercise contract. Escalation default unchanged (its input is `phase_state.json` + the active output files; PRD/verification are not on its read path).

  *Docs:* `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` documents every new field on `current_phase.json`. `autodev/docs/ASSUMPTIONS.md` adds §F (project-type strict-mode rationale), §G (launch/switch plumbing decision), and §H (additive `exit_criteria_block` rationale). `SETUP.md` documents the three-pillar contract (prd.md / roadmap.md / verification.md) at the top of "What Testers Bring."

  *Tests added (new files):* `tests/test_p0_validate_verification.py` (16 tests), `tests/test_p0_validate_roadmap_behavioral.py` (10), `tests/test_p0_validate_verification_endpoint.py` (3), `autodev/tests/test_phase_resolver_behavioral.py` (8), `autodev/tests/test_webhook_client_default_messages.py` (4). New test classes appended in-place to `tests/test_api_setup_preflight.py` (+8), `tests/test_api_setup_launch.py` (+3), `tests/test_api_setup_switch_project.py` (+1), `tests/test_ui_preflight_screen.py` (+3), `tests/test_roadmap_parser.py` (+3 incl. drift detector). Existing fixtures in `test_api_setup_validate_roadmap.py`, `test_defensive_c3_05.py`, and `test_queue_api.py` updated to include the now-required Behavioral Verification block + `verification_content` body field. Final: `pytest tests/ -q` 1670 passed / 22 skipped; `pytest autodev/tests/ -q` 600 passed (2 pre-existing `test_defensive_c3_07.py` failures verified to exist on `main` before this change — unrelated).

### Changed

- **Ideas chat conversation context (bounded prompt window + master log):** `ui/server.py` no longer prepends the entire `[CONVERSATION HISTORY]` block to every `POST /api/ideas/{id}/message` webhook (the old behavior grew the prompt linearly with thread length and degraded `prd-creator` output once chats passed ~8–10 turns). The server now injects at most the last **3 complete (user, assistant) pairs** inline, hard-capped by `AUTODEV_IDEAS_HISTORY_CHAR_BUDGET` (default `20000`). Older pairs collapse into a single `[NOTE] N earlier turn(s) omitted…` line that points the agent at `~/.openclaw/ideas/{id}/conversation_log.md`. That log is server-owned, append-only, written atomically after every successful turn, idempotent per `## Turn N` marker, and lazily bootstrapped from `session.json` for ideas created before this change. Timeout (`408`) and webhook-failure rollback paths do not write to the log. `autodev/agents/prd-creator/AGENTS.md` gains a **Conversation History Access** section explaining when (and only when) the agent should `Read` the log. New tests: `tests/test_api_ideas_conversation_log.py`, `tests/test_api_ideas_history_window.py`.

- **Install (`install.sh`):** Removed the OpenClaw `openclaw.json` **version** advisory check and the matching summary row. The script is **14 steps** (steps renumbered); behavior of all other steps is unchanged.

- **Pipeline Monitor Current Phase:** Removed the monospace **`last_error_code`** chip and the **skill / agent** line under **Agent attempts** (they read as live failures and duplicated queue/metrics). **`last_error_code`** and **`skill_*`** remain on **`GET /api/state`** and in queue snapshots. **`gate_fail`** lines in **`pipeline_events.jsonl`** now include **`last_error_code`** from **`phase_state`** at emit time; the Activity feed **`humanizeSummary`** path appends the existing H-23 long titles via **`getLastErrorCodeTitle`**, with the same text on the row **`title`** when present. See **`autodev/tests/test_gate_fail_event_last_error_code.py`**.

### Added

- **`AUTODEV_IDEAS_HISTORY_CHAR_BUDGET` env var** (default `20000`) bounds the inline `[CONVERSATION HISTORY]` block in Ideas chat webhooks. Discoverable via `.env.example` and the install-time hint block written by the new `ensure_dotenv_ideas_history_budget_hint()` helper in `autodev/installer/setup_helpers.py` (uses an independent `DOTENV_IDEAS_HISTORY_BUDGET_HINT_MARKER` so existing installs with the older idle-hints block still gain the placeholder on next `install.sh` run). `install.sh` step 10/14 now runs both ideas hint helpers.

- **`.env.example` stall-timeout block corrected:** replaced the stale `planner 900 / executor 1800 / reviewer 900` defaults (pre-two-knob-split) with the live values (`STALL_TIMEOUT_* = 300 s`, `STARTUP_GRACE_* = 600 s`) and added the previously missing `AUTODEV_STARTUP_GRACE_PLANNER/EXECUTOR/REVIEWER` placeholders so the template matches what `ensure_dotenv_stall_timeout_hints()` writes into live `.env` files. A new test `test_env_example_includes_every_helper_placeholder` in `autodev/tests/test_setup_helpers.py` enforces this parity going forward.

- **Executor session abort on retry:** Before each executor webhook for attempt N+1 (`executor_retries` > 0), `orchestrator.py` calls `abort_agent_session()` in `webhook_client.py`, which opens the OpenClaw Gateway WebSocket (`/__openclaw__/ws`), handshakes with `gateway.auth.token`, and sends RPC `sessions.abort` for the previous session key (`…executor-attempt-N`). This is best-effort (logs and continues on failure). `load_config()` now exposes `gateway_token` and `gateway_ws_url` from `openclaw.json` (`gateway.port` default 18789). `websocket-client==1.2.3` is pinned in `ui/requirements.txt`.

- **Ideas PRD vs roadmap staleness guard:** `GET /api/ideas/{id}/draft-sync-status` returns `roadmap_behind_prd` plus draft file mtimes. Project Ideas **Continue to Setup →** calls it first; when the saved PRD file is newer than the saved roadmap draft, a modal suggests regenerating (with **Continue anyway** persisting a per-mtime **sessionStorage** dismiss under `autodev:roadmapBehindPrdDismissed:<ideaId>`).

- **Stall-timeout env discovery:** `.env.example` includes a commented **Tier A stall timeouts** section (`AUTODEV_STALL_TIMEOUT_*`; no live values). After merging canonical keys, `install.sh` calls `ensure_dotenv_stall_timeout_hints()` so existing `.env` files gain the same commented block once (marker line prevents duplicates). SETUP.md cross-links the behavior.

- **Ideas UI poll env discovery:** `.env.example` includes commented **`AUTODEV_IDEAS_IDLE_THRESHOLD`** and **`AUTODEV_IDEAS_STARTUP_GRACE`** (defaults idle 300 s / grace 30 s — idle was raised from the original 120 s; see the timeout-threshold Fixed entries). `install.sh` calls `ensure_dotenv_ideas_idle_hints()` with the same idempotent marker pattern as stall timeouts.

- **`autodev-pipeline-signals` plugin** (`autodev/plugin/`): OpenClaw TypeScript plugin registering hooks:
  - `agent_end` — fires fire-and-forget after every session closes; writes the missing `{agent}_output.done` sentinel when the agent did not write one itself (dead-on-arrival, crash, provider rejection). Unblocks `poll_for_sentinel` immediately rather than waiting for the hard timeout.
  - `before_agent_finalize` — fires before the harness accepts the agent's final answer; checks `planner_output.json`, `executor_output.json`, and `reviewer_output.json` for structural completeness and returns `{ action: "revise" }` if required fields are absent. Requests a bounded in-context correction pass without burning an orchestrator retry. File-system, git, and subprocess checks remain in the hard gate scripts.
  - **Tier A stall detection:** the orchestrator seeds `{agent}_activity.stamp` before each planner/executor/reviewer webhook, then `model_call_started`, `model_call_ended`, and `after_tool_call` refresh it in the pipeline artifacts directory. The plugin also subscribes to OpenClaw's live agent event stream so activity is recorded even when typed hook context omits `agentId`, `sessionKey`, or `workspaceDir`. `poll_for_sentinel` reads the stamp mtime each tick; if silence exceeds `AUTODEV_STALL_TIMEOUT_PLANNER` (default 900 s), `AUTODEV_STALL_TIMEOUT_EXECUTOR` (1800 s), or `AUTODEV_STALL_TIMEOUT_REVIEWER` (900 s), the poll returns `False` and existing retry paths run. `cleanup_output_files` removes `*_activity.stamp` each attempt.
  - **Ideas (`prd-creator`):** same hooks touch **`{OPENCLAW_ROOT}/ideas/{ideaId}/prd_creator_activity.stamp`** for any `ideas:` session key; `agent_end` for **`ideas:{id}:session-{n}`** writes **`turns/{n}.done`** when missing so the UI sentinel poll unblocks without waiting for JSONL ticks. The UI server deletes the stamp before each Ideas webhook (mirrors pipeline stamp cleanup) and polls using stamp ``st_mtime_ns`` (`tests/test_ideas_stamp_detect.py`).
  - Install: `openclaw plugins install <repo>/autodev/plugin`; `openclaw.json` entry requires `hooks.allowConversationAccess: true`. `install.sh` step 11/14 handles this automatically and validates the installed hooks with `openclaw plugins inspect autodev-pipeline-signals --json`.
  - Plugin tests: `autodev/plugin/tests/agent-end.test.ts`, `autodev/plugin/tests/before-finalize.test.ts`, `autodev/plugin/tests/stall-detector.test.ts` (46 tests total, node:test with tsx).

- **Heartbeat cron is now a silent self-healer:** `autodev/pipeline/heartbeat_cron.py` no longer sends any Signal notifications. The model-query path (`query_heartbeat_model`, `HEARTBEAT_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `send_signal_notification`) has been removed entirely. All recovery decisions are now fully deterministic: idle/terminal states (IDLE, PIPELINE_COMPLETE, STOPPED, QUEUE_HALTED, HALTED_SILENT, BLOCKED, WAITING_FOR_HUMAN) cause a log-and-exit with no action; only stale RUNNING/WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL (> 15 min with lock free) triggers an automatic orchestrator restart. The escalation agent remains the sole owner of all human-facing notifications. `requests` import removed.

### Removed

- **Alignment and adversarial secondary checks (MVP cleanup):** `POST /api/ideas/{id}/alignment-check` and `POST /api/ideas/{id}/adversarial-check` endpoints removed with all associated helpers (`_notify_prd_agent`, `_count_alignment_gaps`, `_extract_adversarial_confidence`, `_extract_adversarial_top_risk`) and timeout constants. The Ideas screen Alignment and Adversarial document tabs, the post-roadmap quality check modal (`ideas-roadmap-generated-modal`), and all related React state are removed. `alignment_report` and `adversarial_report` are filtered from `GET /api/ideas/{id}/session` responses; existing stored values are preserved on disk. Alignment and adversarial skill SKILL.md files are retained in `autodev/skill-library/`.

### Fixed

- **Escalation NameError crash (`_PIPELINE_ARTIFACTS` → `PROJECT_ARTIFACTS_DIR`):** `orchestrator.py` referenced the undefined name `_PIPELINE_ARTIFACTS` at two points in the escalation webhook message construction (repo-init-failure path line 2862 and blame-cap path line 4132). At runtime this raised `NameError`, crashing the orchestrator before the escalation webhook could fire and before phase metrics were finalised — the proximate cause of the "5 vs 3 attempt count" discrepancy visible in the UI. Both occurrences replaced with the correct module-level constant `PROJECT_ARTIFACTS_DIR`. `webhook_client.py`'s own `_PIPELINE_ARTIFACTS` (the workspace-relative agent-facing path string) is unchanged.

- **`ERR_UNACCOUNTED_DELETION` false positive on vite content-hash bundle rotation:** `executor_gate.py` now distinguishes vite content-hashed build artifact rotation from genuine unaccounted file deletion. Files under `dist/assets/` matching the pattern `*-{6–12 alphanum chars}.{js|css}` are auto-accounted when the parent directory still contains at least one file with the same extension after the build (proving rotation, not wipe). A `[GATE WARN]` line is emitted to stderr listing the auto-accounted paths; the gate passes. All other deletion checks — source files, non-hash-named dist files, wipes with no replacement — behave identically to before. This eliminates the systematic failure loop that caused GAME-E1, DATA-E3, and UI-E4 to exhaust executor retries whenever a phase ran `npm run build` as part of test verification.

- **`_ideas_scrub_stale_turn_artifacts` tolerance gap:** The scrub now uses the same `IDEAS_LATE_DONE_MTIME_SLACK_SEC` (2 s) boundary as `_late_done_valid_for_attempt` when deciding whether to delete a prior `turns/{n}.done` sentinel. Previously, a `.done` written just before `attempt_start_wall` could be removed after the webhook POST but before the poll, causing spurious `408` responses from `POST /api/ideas/{id}/message`.

- **Inference provider rejections (`ERR_PROVIDER_REJECTED`):** Renamed `ERR_PROVIDER_QUOTA` to `ERR_PROVIDER_REJECTED` for clearer operator-facing semantics. The orchestrator now re-reads session JSONL after planner and reviewer gate failures (in addition to post-poll paths) so late-flushed OpenRouter-style billing/auth errors escalate immediately without burning agent retry budget; reviewer paths now call the same detection as planner/executor. The dashboard `LastErrorCode` chip shows an explicit title for `ERR_PROVIDER_REJECTED` and `ERR_SESSION_DEAD_ON_ARRIVAL`.

- **`GET /api/ideas/{id}/session` stale roadmap:** when `roadmap_draft.done` exists and `roadmap_draft.md` differs from `session.json` `roadmap_content` (regenerate wrote disk but session was not updated), rehydration now replaces session from disk so the Ideas Roadmap tab matches the converter output.

- **`POST /api/ideas/{id}/message` stale roadmap:** loading session for a PRD turn now runs the same roadmap draft merge before pre-save and again before the final write, so sending chat after a regenerate no longer persists outdated `roadmap_content` when only `roadmap_draft.md` was updated.

- **`POST /api/ideas/{id}/convert` stale sentinel:** removes pre-existing `roadmap_draft.done` after the webhook POST and before polling (same behavior as `fix-roadmap-format`), so **Regenerate Roadmap** no longer returns immediately with the previous `roadmap_draft.md` while the new converter run is still in flight.

### Changed

- **Project Queue header:** Removed the aggregate summary line (ready / blocked / on-hold / complete counts). **Progression** (Auto / Manual) and **Run next project** (manual mode) sit in a right-aligned cluster with `data-testid="queue-header-trailing"`; fetch errors stay with the primary left group (title, add, reorder).

- **Project Ideas:** **Generate Roadmap** / **Regenerate Roadmap** stay disabled while a chat reply is in progress (same `isLoading` path as **Send**), with a short inline hint so operators know conversion waits for the latest PRD. `doConvert`, `beginConvertAfterGuards`, and `_runConvert` guard against starting conversion during that window (including low-readiness and regenerate confirm paths).

- **Tests:** `test_installer_register_agent.py` section markers updated to match **`install.sh`** comment headers (`3/14` … `4/14`, `5/14` … `6/14`, `8/14` … `9/14`).

- **Ideas UI poll (`readiness/poll` completion):** after the background readiness job finishes and the client loads ``GET /api/ideas/{id}/readiness``, the UI calls ``refreshIdeas()`` so the sidebar **X/10** badge matches the PRD readiness strip without switching ideas.

- **Ideas roadmap generation timeout recovery:** if ``POST /api/ideas/{id}/convert`` times out but the converter writes ``roadmap_draft.md`` shortly after, the active Roadmap tab now polls ``GET /api/ideas/{id}/session`` and fills ``roadmapContent`` without requiring operators to click away and back.

- **Ideas screen selection after reload:** the last-opened idea id is stored in ``sessionStorage`` (``autodev:lastIdeaId``) when selecting from the rail or creating a new idea, restored on load when still present in ``GET /api/ideas``, and cleared when deleting that active idea or when the stored id is no longer listed. Falls back to the first list entry when absent or stale.

- **``GET /api/ideas``:** responses include ``Cache-Control: no-store`` to avoid browser-cached idea lists; sort uses ``(updated, id)`` descending so equal timestamps order deterministically.

- **Ideas blocking poll (`_poll_sentinel_with_idle_detect`):** activity is tracked with ``st_mtime_ns`` instead of float ``getmtime``, so rapid plugin stamp touches in the same wall-clock second still reset the idle window. Default ``poll_timeout`` / ``POLL_TIMEOUT`` is **600s** (was 180s). For ``poll_timeout >= 180``, the effective ``ideas_idle_threshold`` is floored at **60s** so a mis-set ultra-low idle value cannot abort a healthy run before the hard deadline. **``POST /api/ideas/.../message``** and **``_notify_prd_agent``** call the poller with ``use_stamp_idle=False`` so only the hard deadline gates completion (stamp ``idle`` / ``no_session`` caused false 408s while the agent was still running).

- **Ideas gateway + session UX:** OpenClaw webhook POST for Ideas uses a **120s** aiohttp total timeout (was 10s) to reduce false **503** when the gateway is slow. **``GET /api/ideas/{id}/session``** always returns **``Cache-Control: no-store``** (including default empty sessions for new ideas). On **502/503**, the UI starts the same **late-heal** session poll as **408**; the server **rolls back** the pre-saved user + pending-assistant pair so failed gateway attempts are not persisted, while the client shows a short error, restores **text + attachment + notes** into the composer, and drops the ephemeral pair on successful resubmit.

- **`poll_for_sentinel` stall detection:** optional `stall_detection_path` and `stall_threshold_seconds`; when the activity stamp mtime is older than the threshold, returns `False` (same contract as stop/timeout) so planner/executor/reviewer retry paths run. `cleanup_output_files` deletes `{prefix}_activity.stamp`, and `initialize_activity_stamp()` seeds a fresh empty stamp at attempt start so missing first hook events are still timed.

- **`poll_for_sentinel` now accepts `min_sentinel_mtime`:** The stale-sentinel guard previously in `poll_for_sentinel_with_idle_detect` is now a parameter of `poll_for_sentinel`. Capture `time.time()` before `cleanup_output_files()` and pass it as `min_sentinel_mtime=` to discard orphaned sentinels from a prior session that wrote their `.done` file after a reset.

- **Idle detection machinery removed** from `sentinel_poller.py` and `orchestrator.py`:
  - `_latest_activity_mtime`, `poll_for_sentinel_with_idle_detect` removed from `sentinel_poller.py`.
  - `_watch_root_for_idle_detect` removed from `orchestrator.py`.
  - All 4 `poll_for_sentinel_with_idle_detect` call sites (executor, reviewer, completion review × 2) replaced with `poll_for_sentinel`. `ui/server.py` completion review call updated likewise.
  - `poll_for_sentinel` is now a simple timeout backstop; the `agent_end` plugin hook provides the authoritative completion signal.

- **JSONL session-lookup loops simplified** (orchestrator.py): The 15-retry loops (~30 s) that waited for `sessions.json` to be populated before the sentinel poll have been replaced with a single post-sentinel read. `agent_end` is guaranteed to fire after `sessions.json` is updated, so the retry loop is unnecessary.

- **Dead-session check moved post-sentinel** (orchestrator.py, executor and reviewer branches): `_check_session_dead_on_arrival` now runs after `poll_for_sentinel` returns instead of before it. `sessions.json` is reliably populated by the time `agent_end` unblocks the sentinel poll, so the single-attempt check is always accurate.

### Removed

- **`autodev/agents/escalation/HEARTBEAT.md`** (repo source and `~/.openclaw/workspace-escalation/HEARTBEAT.md` runtime copy) deleted. The escalation agent's notification behaviour is governed by its `AGENTS.md` and triggered by the orchestrator — no `HEARTBEAT.md` is needed or correct. Planner/executor/reviewer `HEARTBEAT.md` files remain empty — those agents do not send notifications.
- **`query_heartbeat_model`, `send_signal_notification`, `HEARTBEAT_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `HEARTBEAT_MODEL_URL`, `HEARTBEAT_MODEL_NAME`** removed from `heartbeat_cron.py`. The model query was the source of false-positive Signal notifications for normal idle states (IDLE, PIPELINE_COMPLETE, STOPPED). Decision logic is now deterministic only.
- **`poll_for_sentinel_with_idle_detect`** and `_latest_activity_mtime` from `autodev/pipeline/sentinel_poller.py`.
- **`_watch_root_for_idle_detect`** from `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`.

- **Dead-session detection:** `_check_session_dead_on_arrival()` escalates immediately when an OpenClaw session records `runtimeMs == 0` + `stopReason == "error"` (provider rejected before any work). Applied to both executor and reviewer. See `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_dead_session.py`.

### Changed

- **Symlink pre-webhook auto-reconcile:** Before executor and reviewer webhooks, `_verify_symlinks_consistent(project_path, self.update_symlink)` detects when `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT/pipeline-project` and `OPENCLAW_ROOT/pipeline-project` disagree with `pipeline_state["project_path"]`. On divergence it invokes `update_symlink` to repoint both symlinks (Policy A — state wins), logs `[RECONCILE]`, and re-verifies; logs `[WARN]` / `[ERROR]` and returns `False` if `project_path` is empty, reconciliation fails, or divergence persists. With no `symlink_fixer` argument the function remains warn-only. The return value does not gate webhook invocation. See `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_symlink_consistency.py`.

- **Reviewer sentinel timeout capped at 3:** Reviewer branch now escalates to `"escalation"` agent after 3 consecutive sentinel timeouts (mirrors planner). `write_failure_context` called on every timeout so operators always see current reviewer failure data. See `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_reviewer_timeout_cap.py`.

- **Reviewer sentinel** now uses `poll_for_sentinel` (post-agent_end integration). The previous `poll_for_sentinel_with_idle_detect` call and JSONL session-lookup loop have been replaced; see the agent_end entry above. See `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_reviewer_sentinel.py`.

- **Preflight: repo with no commits now attempts initial commit:** Both fresh-init and existing-repo paths in `_run_preflight_checks` attempt `git add -A && git commit` (falling back to `--allow-empty`) when `HEAD` is absent. Emits `"fixed"` on success, `"fail"` on failure (blocking queue-add). Prevents `ERR_MISSING_BASE_COMMIT` at pipeline start. See `ui/server.py`, `tests/test_api_setup_preflight.py`.

- **Agent ladder: no green dots during terminal halt + escalation:** `getAgentAttemptDotStates` escalation branch now guards `completedLeg()` with `terminalNoBlue.has(ps)`, using `activeRow(x, false)` instead for `HALTED_SILENT` / `BLOCKED` / `STOPPED` / `QUEUE_HALTED` / `PIPELINE_COMPLETE`. Fixes misleading green slots contradicting the INTERVENTION REQUIRED pill. See `ui/index.html`, `tests/test_ui_current_phase_panel.py`.

- **Merge failure reason includes stderr:** Git merge failure now captures `merge_result.stderr` and uses it as the escalation transition reason instead of a static string. See `autodev/pipeline/orchestrator.py`, `autodev/tests/test_orchestrator_merge_stderr.py`.

- **Reviewer model:** Updated from Qwen3.5-27B (llama-server) to MiniMax M2.7 via OpenRouter. See `autodev/agents/reviewer/IDENTITY.md`.

- **Spec drift fixed:** `PIPELINE-SPEC.md` §ERR_UNACCOUNTED_DELETION corrected — `phase_base_commit` absent is fail-closed (`ERR_MISSING_BASE_COMMIT`), not a skipped warning. `CLAUDE.md` operational constants updated: executor sentinel 1200 s, reviewer 600 s, idle threshold 300 s.

- **Chat UI Milestone 4 (UI-8, UI-9):** **Hover card** — `ReactDOM.createPortal` to `document.body`, **320 ms** show delay, **100 ms** dismiss bridge (row → card), `data-idea-hover-row` + scroll/resize sync for `top`; card shows summary or **“New project — no documentation yet.”** (em dash), **PRD Readiness** bar row (tier-colored bar + `N/10`, or em dash when score null), **PRD** / **Roadmap** rows with outline SVG icons and **✓** / **—** states. **Inline list badges** — `readiness_score` as **`N/10`** (`text-[10px]`, emerald ≥8 / amber 5–7 / red &lt;5), omitted when null. **UI-9:** removed **Assessing…** and header **score** from the Ideas chat header (PRD tab body readiness unchanged). Tests: [`tests/test_ui_hover_card.py`](tests/test_ui_hover_card.py), [`tests/test_ui_chat_header_score.py`](tests/test_ui_chat_header_score.py), [`tests/test_ui_m3_frontend.py`](tests/test_ui_m3_frontend.py). Inline list score display ships in **Milestone 4 (UI-8)** on top of this API. ### Added

- **B-04 — Orchestrator crash context:** `GET /api/state` includes `orchestrator_spawn_log_tail` (up to five lines) when `orchestrator_alive` is false and `pipeline_status` is `RUNNING` or `WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL`, read from the same path the UI uses for spawned orchestrator stdout/stderr (`ORCHESTRATOR_SPAWN_LOG_PATH`, default `/tmp/orchestrator.log`). Pipeline Monitor **Orchestrator stopped** panel renders those lines in a `<pre>` (`data-testid="pipeline-orchestrator-spawn-log-tail"`). `POST /api/resume-orchestrator` behavior is unchanged (still returns 200 after a successful spawn without waiting for liveness). See `tests/test_api_state.py`.
- `SECURITY.md` — security model, vulnerability reporting process, scope, and known limitations
- `CONTRIBUTING.md` — development setup, test commands, PR conventions, skill authoring guidance
- `CHANGELOG.md` — this file
- `autodev/pipeline/env_resolvers.py` — shared resolvers (`resolve_openclaw_root`, `resolve_pipeline_root`) used by orchestrator, heartbeat cron, gate scripts, skill/session/sentinel managers, and `ui/server.py`. Canonical env names are `OPENCLAW_ROOT` and `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT`.
- UI JSON key `autodev_pipeline_root` in `ui/config.json`.
- Startup INFO log in `orchestrator.py` and `heartbeat_cron.py`: a single line emitting the resolved `OPENCLAW_ROOT`, `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT`, and `STATE_FILE` so an operator can self-diagnose misconfigured roots without grepping env dumps.

### Changed

- **Ideas chat sentinel poll (`ui/server.py`):** `_poll_sentinel_with_idle_detect` now uses **`prd_creator_activity.stamp`** mtime (plugin-maintained) instead of JSONL mtime and scanning `prd_draft.md` / `turns/*.md`. Removed `_resolve_prd_creator_jsonl`, `_idea_workspace_activity_mtime`, and dead `ORCHESTRATOR_POLL_TIMEOUT`. `test_ideas_idle_detect.py` removed in favour of `tests/test_ideas_stamp_detect.py`.

- **Ideas — Roadmap generated modal:** The post–**Generate Roadmap** success dialog (`showQualityCheckModal`) now uses three short paragraphs plus slightly looser vertical spacing for the top copy so next-step guidance is easier to scan (`ui/index.html`, `IdeasScreen`).
- **P1-02 + L-26 — Queue manual “Run next project”:** In **Project Queue** manual mode, the former **Trigger Next** control is renamed **`Run next project`** (`data-testid="queue-trigger-next"`). When disabled, a native **`title`** tooltip explains the first blocking reason via **`getRunNextProjectDisabledReason`** (starting request, active queue row, unsaved reorder, or busy pipeline status). When enabled, the button uses the existing **`run-pulse`** animation for emphasis. See `ui/index.html` (`QueueScreen`) and `tests/test_ui_p1_02_l26_queue_run_next.py`; tracker `plans/Active/ux-friction-2026-04-16.md`.
- **P0-03 — Preflight step track:** Setup & Preflight uses three numbered step cards (`data-testid="preflight-step-1"` … `preflight-step-3`) with gray / emerald / red / amber borders matching repo lock, roadmap lock, and preflight outcome. A `preflight-launch-blocking-hint` line names the first incomplete step when **Launch** is disabled. Path confirm button is **Confirm repository**; per-step helper copy replaces the old single combined paragraph (tracker **L-13–L-16**). **Run Preflight** lives in step 3; **Add to Queue** / **Launch Now** / **Back** sit in a separate row below. See `ui/index.html` (`PreflightScreen`) and `tests/test_ui_p0_03_preflight_step_track.py`.
- **P0-02 — Orchestrator recovery banner & escalation gating:** Pipeline Monitor shows a full-width header **recovery banner** (`data-testid="pipeline-orchestrator-recovery-banner"`) with **Restart Orchestrator** when `orchestrator_alive` is false and `pipeline_status` is **`WAITING_FOR_HUMAN`**, **`STOPPED`**, or **`QUEUE_HALTED`** with escalation context (`escalation_trigger_reason` / `escalation_message`). Mid-flight **`RUNNING`** / **`WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL`** + dead orchestrator remains in the left-column **Orchestrator stopped** panel (B-04). **EscalationCommandPanel** hides the command grid until the orchestrator is alive (`orchestratorDownBlocksCommands`); copy points to the banner. Plain **`IDLE`** + dead does not show the banner (normal idle). See `ui/index.html` and `tests/test_ui_p0_02_orchestrator_recovery_banner.py`.
- **P0-01 — Cold-start default screen:** After setup is complete, the UI runs a one-time bootstrap (`Promise.all` on `GET /api/state` and `GET /api/queue`). If `pipeline_status` is **`IDLE`** or **`UNKNOWN`** (e.g. missing `pipeline_state.json`) and no queue entry has a busy `live_pipeline_status` (`RUNNING`, `WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL`, `WAITING_FOR_HUMAN`), the app navigates to **Project Ideas** instead of landing on an empty Pipeline Monitor. **`PIPELINE_COMPLETE` and other statuses do not trigger this redirect** — a completed pipeline with an empty queue stays on the Pipeline Monitor (completion view) after refresh. Skips redirect if the user already left Pipeline before the fetch completes (`currentScreenRef`). See `ui/index.html` (`shouldOpenIdeasOnColdBootstrap`, `App` bootstrap `useEffect`) and `tests/test_ui_cold_start_default_screen.py`.
- **`POST /api/resume-orchestrator` (Policy A — B-01):** When the configured pipeline-project symlink realpath disagrees with `pipeline_state.json` `project_path`, the UI server **repoints the symlink** to match state instead of returning **409** `symlink_project_mismatch`. Successful responses include `reconciled`, `reconcile_action`, `previous_symlink_real`, and `canonical_project_real`. **422** if the link path cannot be safely replaced (e.g. real directory or file at `project_dir_path`). **503** returns a JSON body with `reconciled: true` if spawn fails after a successful repoint (operator can retry **Restart**). **409** is reserved for `pipeline.lock` / orchestrator already running. Pipeline Monitor shows a single amber header line for reconcile / spawn-after-reconcile failures (`ui/index.html`). Failed **Resume** / **Restart Orchestrator** surfaces map known API messages to operator-facing copy on the client (L-38). See `autodev/docs/PIPELINE-SPEC.md` and `tests/test_api_resume_orchestrator.py`.
- Environment variable rename (hard cut — legacy aliases removed):
  - `AUTODEV_ROOT` → `OPENCLAW_ROOT`
  - `AUTODEV_RUNTIME_ROOT` → `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT`
  - `install.sh` writes only the canonical names to `.env`.
  - `.env.example` rewritten to document canonical names only.
- `RUNTIME-MIGRATION.md`, `SETUP.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `SECURITY.md`, pipeline docs, and helper shell scripts (`scripts/queue-e2e-*.sh`, `scripts/README.md`) updated to reference canonical names only.
- Pipeline Monitor: removed the header liveness dot and the **Restart Orchestrator** control from the primary header **button row** (Resume / Stop cluster). When the orchestrator is down during a mid-flight run (`RUNNING` / `WAITING_FOR_SENTINEL`), restart is offered in the **Current Phase** column (**Orchestrator stopped** panel, B-04). When down in **`WAITING_FOR_HUMAN`**, **`STOPPED`**, or **`QUEUE_HALTED`** with escalation context, restart is offered in the **recovery banner** under the header (P0-02); the escalation command grid stays hidden until `orchestrator_alive` is true.
- `install.sh` startup echo updated to recommend loopback binding (`127.0.0.1`) rather than `0.0.0.0`

### Removed

- `AUTODEV_USE_LEGACY_OPENCLAW_RUNTIME` environment flag and `use_legacy_openclaw_runtime` UI config key. Both are silently ignored if they remain in stale `.env` / `config.json`. To reproduce the old behaviour, set `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT=$OPENCLAW_ROOT` explicitly. The flag's branching logic has been removed from `orchestrator.py`, `heartbeat_cron.py`, gate scripts, and `ui/server.py`.
- Legacy env-var aliases `AUTODEV_ROOT` and `AUTODEV_RUNTIME_ROOT` and the UI config alias `autodev_runtime_root`. The resolver functions in `autodev/pipeline/env_resolvers.py`, the `load_config()` layer in `ui/server.py`, the `_spawn_orchestrator` env builder (which now actively scrubs inherited legacy aliases), the `.env` writer in `install.sh`, and the pipeline module constants (`orchestrator.py`, `sentinel_poller.py`, `skill_manager.py`, `session_cleanup.py`, `heartbeat_cron.py`) have all been updated to read and emit only canonical names. Stale aliases in an inherited environment or an existing `.env` / `ui/config.json` are silently ignored. To migrate, replace any `AUTODEV_ROOT=` / `AUTODEV_RUNTIME_ROOT=` entries with `OPENCLAW_ROOT=` / `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT=`.

### Fixed

- **Ideas ``POST /message``:** removes orphan ``turns/{n}.done`` and paired ``{n}.md`` when their mtime predates the current attempt window (same slack as late-done reconcile), so the sentinel poll cannot return immediately on stale disk artifacts and attach a prior turn's prose to a new user message.

- `load_config()` / `ui/config.json`: keys present with empty-string values (as in `config.example.json`) no longer count as user overrides, so `_finalize_autodev_config_paths` still derives `pipeline_state_path`, `project_dir_path`, and related runtime paths. Fixes “pipeline_state_path is not configured” after install when placeholders were left in the file.
- `repo_init_check.py` resolves `pipeline-project` with the same runtime-root rules as the orchestrator and `phase_resolver` (default: `$AUTODEV_REPO_PATH/.autodev/pipeline-project`). Fixes false “symlink not found” checks against `~/.openclaw` when using repo-local runtime.
- Orchestrator atomic writes (`phase_state`, `failure_context`, related `mkstemp` paths) fall back to the pipeline root (with `makedirs`) instead of the OpenClaw root when `pipeline-project` is missing, avoiding an uncaught `FileNotFoundError` if `~/.openclaw` was never created (e.g. Docker `OPENCLAW_ROOT` not loaded from `.env`).
- `_spawn_orchestrator` no longer clobbers `AUTODEV_PIPELINE_ROOT` in the child process environment when the UI config value is empty — the parent environment is preserved instead. Same fix applied for `OPENCLAW_ROOT` propagation.
- `/home/pi/` author paths replaced with neutral placeholders across UI, tests, and docs
- `.claude/settings.json` excluded from git tracking
- `requests` pinned to exact version in dependencies

### Security

- `aiohttp` bumped from 3.13.3 to 3.13.4, resolving 10 CVEs
